Those who have experienced Mount Hua even a little know this: no matter what anyone says, Chung Myung represents Mount Hua. Whether seen from outside or inside, Chung Myung wields an incredible influence over the Mount Hua sect.

But those who have been close to Mount Hua for a long time see things a bit differently.

Chung Myung may be the center of the Mount Hua sect, but the core among its disciples is Baek Cheon. Chung Myung merely leads them, but the one who harmonizes their thoughts and practically controls them is this earnest young man in front of them, no matter what anyone says.

Even Hyun Jong, the sect leader of Mount Hua, when he has something to convey to the disciples, goes through Baek Cheon rather than Chung Myung.

It’s not just a second choice because Chung Myung is busy. While Chung Myung leads Mount Hua’s grand direction, it is an implicit agreement that Baek Cheon must lead the disciples.

Baek Cheon, the confirmed future sect leader of Mount Hua and representative of the current disciples, suddenly bowed his head before them.

Tang Pae looked around with a blank face. No one else was different; everyone was too stunned to speak.

Perhaps because Tang Pae was the only one to move, or because he was seen as the right person, all eyes turned to him.

Under implicit pressure, Tang Pae finally spoke with a reluctant expression.

“Taoist... why are you suddenly being like this?”

Only then did Baek Cheon raise his bowed head and evenly look at Tang Pae and everyone else.

At that moment, many words came to Baek Cheon’s mind: polite words, gentle coaxing, deeper apologies to make the other feel sorry, and so on.

But all those vanished in an instant. This was not a situation to patch up with mere face-saving.

“This cannot continue.”

What came out was his honest feeling.

“Right now, we are training against absolute warriors whom we cannot serve anywhere else. Every moment is invaluable beyond comparison.”

Everyone nodded at his words.

Whether enduring this training is hard or not was secondary; everyone agreed this was a rare opportunity unlikely to come again.

Even the disciples of the Sichuan Tang family rarely get a chance to face the family lord. Has the Beast Palace’s archer ever had a serious fight with the Beast Palace Lord?

Where else would one experience a situation where not one but three such figures rampage?

“But right now, we are wasting this time. This opportunity may never come again.”

“...”

“Thinking of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and even the murim world, honestly, I don’t understand such grand ideas well. But I don’t want to waste this precious time like this. When we should focus all our strength on facing them, I don’t want to mix in old grudges.”

Some, especially Namgung Do-Wei, nodded strongly.

“So I ask you, please unite your strength.”

Tang Pae spoke with a slightly displeased face.

“Is that because it’s the path for the greater good?”

“No.”

Baek Cheon said bluntly.

“Because it’s the path that benefits Mount Hua.”

Baek Cheon’s gaze fixed on Tang Pae.

“Isn’t that the same for young lord?”

Tang Pae’s mouth tightened slightly.

Baek Cheon was asking him now: does he want the other disciples to waste this great opportunity just enduring punishment because of petty grudges? Or does he want them to use this time to improve themselves?

‘He speaks well.’

Anger surged instantly. This whole matter started because Mount Hua mocked and beat the Yang family, and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint relentlessly tormented everyone.

But when those who are practically the perpetrators speak so plainly, anger is inevitable.

Murder is wrong. But if the one who committed murder preaches this while being avenged, isn’t it natural to feel sick inside?

“It’s ridiculous for Mount Hua to utter such words.”

Baek Cheon bowed his head deeply again without excuse. Tang Pae, who had needlessly found fault, groaned and sighed.

“But... please don’t think the Taoist is the only one who cares about the disciples of the sect.”

What came to Tang Pae’s mind was none other than Tang Gunak’s gaze.

The words that since things were given without effort, there was no need to be anxious or desperate.

‘Not a single word he said is wrong.’

The ones who should be most desperate now are the Tang family. Despite their superior position, they cannot overcome Mount Hua’s disciples.

So they should be more urgent and restless. Tang Gunak should have done what Baek Cheon is doing now first.

But Tang Pae just lay down, and Mount Hua, who had no reason to be urgent, bowed first.

If you are desperate, you must act like it. Burning inside without moving is not true desperation.

“Regardless of feelings, I agree that we must do something. What they are doing is surely not to just have us get beaten and toughen up.”

Baek Cheon nodded firmly.

“I agree as well.”

At that moment, Namgung Do-Wei quickly spoke.

“If this continues, even after a year nothing will change. We cannot face them if we hinder each other. This cannot be overcome by will or training alone.”

This statement could hurt Namgung Do-Wei’s own pride.

He is practically the lord of the Namgung Family. Saying that the young lords must unite to face the Family lord’s is different from the family lord admitting he cannot face another family lord.

As the lord of the great Namgung Family, known as the greatest family in the world, this was not something to say lightly.

But Namgung Do-Wei accepted his limits willingly. In the past, he would never have admitted it, but now he knew that stubbornly denying his limits would hurt his pride even more.

“There must be a way to fight together. Not hindering each other, but helping. Why not start by finding that?”

Tang Pae nodded firmly at Namgung Do-Wei’s words.

But then.

“How impressive.”

One of the Beast Palace disciples watching the situation sneered openly.

“The three proud sects of Central Plains have made a truly grand decision.”

The three looked at the speaker with stern faces. Before others could speak, Baek Cheon took the lead.

“If you are displeased, I apologize.”

“No, no. What’s there to be displeased about? If the three great sects have decided so, we just follow. How could lowly barbarians oppose that?”

This was not just one twisted person mocking openly. Though only one spoke, the eyes of those behind him were similar. Even the Beast Palace and Ice Palace disciples nodded as if agreeing.

“We did not intend that...”

“Your intentions may be good.”

This time, an Ice Palace disciple spoke.

“But isn’t it Mount Hua, the Tang family, and the Namgung Family who decide the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s direction every time? And all we get is just a message of it.”

“...”

“So we will accept it again this time, but is there a problem?”

Baek Cheon found it difficult to respond to this.

Though Namgung was unfairly lumped in, it was hard to deny that Mount Hua and the Tang family had been deciding the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s affairs, while others were merely informed.

This certainly deviates from the original intent of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

Even if it wasn’t Baek Cheon’s fault, the situation where the Tang family and Namgung naturally stepped forward first in front of everyone was enough to stir up their accumulated grievances.

“We apologize for that part. But we did not intend...”

“No, Taoist Baek Cheon. I don’t understand why you say that when we say we have no complaints. We just follow what is said.”

A sigh escaped Baek Cheon’s lips. He could handle honest attacks, but dealing with such sarcasm was difficult. As Mount Hua’s great disciple, whatever he said would be seen as just playing nice.

While Baek Cheon pondered how to respond, a sharp irritated voice rang out.

“You damn barbarians, mocking us so filthy.”

Instantly, the Ice Palace and Beast Palace disciples flared up and turned their gaze. But upon seeing the speaker, their half-open mouths closed again.

Im So-Byeong, wearing a horribly wrinkled martial robe, barely propping up his upper body, looked at everyone filled with all the irritation he had. Everyone instantly became solemn.

“Have you ever been discriminated against as much as the Evil Sect bastards have? Even before the Namgung Family joined, we were treated like slaves by Mount Hua, and we’re still like this here!”

When Im So-Byeong got angry, the Ice Palace and Beast Palace disciples subtly averted their eyes.

“I am the Green Forest King, you bastards! The Green Forest King! If your palace lord is like this here, you must have endured well, you bastards! Do you see my state?”

With one eye bruised blue, Im So-Byeong openly expressed his anger, and no one dared to speak. If a sect leader of another sect had been treated like that, a war would have broken out immediately.

But Im So-Byeong endured such treatment without complaint. Compared to what Im So-Byeong and Green Forest suffered, the Palaces have been treated like honored guests.

“This is why the Central Plains, Palaces, and humans alike have no shame. You make a fuss asking for care for what you’ve done for the Heavenly Friends Alliance! We’ve been slaves for years and still can’t say such things!”

“...No, well...”

“Ahem.”

Those who almost reflexively wanted to say “Because you’re Evil Sect” hurriedly covered their mouths. That would be no different from saying the Palaces deserve discrimination.

“If you’re going to complain, go get beaten for three years and then talk.”

“...”

“Damn it.”

Exhausted, Im So-Byeong looked up and sighed deeply.

“Taoist Baek Cheon.”

“Yes, Green Forest King.”

“You’ve been beaten enough, haven’t you?”

“...Yes.”

Im So-Byeong spoke with fiery eyes.

“I’m annoyed and don’t want to get beaten anymore, so let’s find a way. It’s no longer fun to watch you get beaten.”

At those words, Baek Cheon slightly curled his lips.

“I share that sentiment.”

“Exactly.”

Im So-Byeong glared with burning eyes at the place where Chung Myung and others had disappeared.

“Let’s show those arrogant fellows how terrifying a rebellion is! Be it the Emperor, family lord, just one spear...”

“S-stop right there!”

That crossed the line, you Evil Sect bastard!

“Everyone, gather!”

At Im So-Byeong’s command, who quickly organized the situation, those scattered began to gather slowly.

Seeing that, Baek Cheon chuckled softly.

‘I did my part, damn it.’

Baek Cheon, silently speaking to Chung Myung who was probably watching somewhere, approached Im So-Byeong with a face as if knowing nothing.

Everyone gathered around Im So-Byeong.

Seeing this, Im So-Byeong smiled with utmost satisfaction. Baek Cheon also smiled contentedly. This scene was proof that the Heavenly Friends Alliance, which had been on a path of conflict and division, was truly beginning to unite as one...

“Why is everyone here? It’s so distracting.”

“...What?”

“The small fry, get lost! Only the representatives gather! Only the representatives!”

Baek Cheon flinched unconsciously and looked around. Even though he knew Im So-Byeong had spoken, he felt a strange déjà vu as if Chung Myung had said it.

“First, the two young lords.”

“Yes.”

“Hmm.”

“Send one representative each from Ice Palace and Beast Palace forward. As for Mount Hua... hmm, just have all five come.”

At that moment, Dang Pae raised his hand.

“What is it?”

“Why five for Mount Hua?”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes twitched at those words.

“I didn’t want to say because there are kids around, but does the young lord of the Sichuan Tang have the confidence to fight those five one-on-one and win?”

Tang Pae quietly closed his mouth.

“Shouldn’t the maximum force be treated differently? Do I even have to explain this?”

“Sorry.”

“Tsk.”

Im So-Byeong fanned his face with his fan as if frustrated.

“And also...”

“Yes?”

“Will the Shaolin representative come too?”

Hae Yeon’s face flushed bright red.

“Um... I think the Green Forest King, the donor, has a bit of a misunderstanding, but I am not the Shaolin representative.”

“Then join as the sixth member of Mount Hua.”

At those words, deep anguish appeared on Hae Yeon’s face.

Being the Shaolin representative doesn’t make sense, but joining as the sixth member of Mount Hua is also very, very strange. After all, isn’t he a monk?

Controlling all the doubts and distractions in his mind, Hae Yeon opened his mouth firmly.

“Donor!”

“Yes?”

“...At least say I am the fifth.”

“...”

“Still, being behind that guy is a bit...”

“Wow... Monk. Why are you doing this to me?”

Jo Gul flared up at Hae Yeon’s blatant gaze, wearing a very wronged expression. But everyone else nodded as if they fully understood Hae Yeon’s feelings.

“Then fifth.”

“No, are we just deciding like this? What about my opinion?”

“Shut up. So noisy.”

“...Such a harsh world.”

Anyway, everyone gathered around Im So-Byeong. After all, he was the only one here with the status to represent a sect. And Im So-Byeong was unofficially recognized as a military strategist of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Naturally, everyone had to listen to him.

But the first words Im So-Byeong said after gathering everyone stunned those who expected a strategy.

“What will you do?”

“...”

“...”

Those gathered looked at Im So-Byeong with various emotions. He fanned himself lightly with a face that seemed to say, ‘So what?’

“Um... Green Forest King.”

“Yes.”

“Didn’t you have a plan...?”

“A plan?”

Im So-Byeong scoffed.

“We’ve just been beaten to death, so where would I have a plan? If I had one, I would’ve used it earlier. Do you think I like getting hit so much that I endured it?”

“...That’s true though...”

When everyone was being polite, the one needed was still Jo Gul.

“No, you’re smart! You must have had some idea, right?”

“Ah, smart?”

“Yes, smart!”

Im So-Byeong scoffed and looked at Jo Gul.

“So you guys have just been getting beaten up, and now you don’t want to get hit more, and you’re too lazy to think, so you want the Evil Sect bastard here to come up with a brilliant plan so you won’t get hit anymore?”

“Th-that’s a bit too much...”

“Because of people like you, strategists die!”

As Im So-Byeong flared up and tried to rush forward, Yu Yiseol and Yoon Jong naturally held him down from both sides. Even though it was the first time, it was surprisingly smooth.

“Strategy? Do these guys think strategy is something you just whip up on the spot? Am I someone who carries a strategy pouch and pulls out plans whenever someone needs it? Hey, you guys! If strategy was that easy, people would treat strategists better! Usually, they ignore us because we can’t use swords and just think with our heads, but when it comes to this, what? If I can’t pull out a strategy in time, I’m useless?”

“Ah, no one said that...”

“These guys think strategists are some kind of immortals! So did Zhuge Liang succeed in the Northern Expedition? Even he couldn’t do it, so why are you making a fuss at me!”

“No one is making a fuss...”

“Damn sword-wielders!”

Those who looked at Im So-Byeong with empty eyes unconsciously turned their heads toward the Green Forest. Unlike the speechless group, the Green Forest members of Green Forest yawned as if used to this situation, thinking ‘That guy’s acting up again.’

“Well... he does that sometimes, so don’t mind him.”

“...”

Everyone suddenly wondered what kind of lives those people had lived.

“Ahem.”

Baek Cheon cleared his throat to lighten the mood.

“So the conclusion is... you don’t really have a plan right now?”

“What if I had a plan?”

“Yes?”

Im So-Byeong said with a nonchalant face.

“If I had a plan, would you follow my orders?”

“Of course...”

Im So-Byeong turned his head and looked at the Beast Palace members.

“You guys are sturdy, so roughly take the hits and grab onto the limbs of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. Of course, your heads might get cracked and limbs broken in the process, but we’ll win.”

Before he finished, the Beast Palace members shouted angrily.

“Why do we have to do that!”

Then Im So-Byeong turned to Baek Cheon with a sullen face.

“Did you hear that?”

Baek Cheon bowed his head in despair.

Thinking about it, except for Mount Hua, other sects had no reason to fully trust Im So-Byeong. Even if they did, they had no reason to bear greater losses to achieve the goal.

‘This is really difficult.’

Baek Cheon realized it anew.

How hard it was to balance the interests and justifications of each sect and unite them. The more he experienced, the more he doubted if it was really possible.

People don’t sacrifice themselves just because of grand causes or walking the right path. If that were possible in reality, why would the world be full of conflicts?

“Tsk tsk.”

Im So-Byeong glanced at Baek Cheon and clicked his tongue. Baek Cheon was about to tilt his head in confusion when Im So-Byeong suddenly spoke.

“Isn’t the answer already out there?”

“Yes?”

“What has the Taoist been doing until now?”

“What have I been doing? Well...”

Hitting, kicking, tearing apart...

“...I’ve only been fighting.”

Suddenly, a wave of guilt surged up.

“Sigh... Why does the Taoist always go only halfway?”

“Yes?”

“Did the Taoist do that because he wanted to? Who is the demon behind all these schemes?”

“Well...”

It’s Chung Myung.

Without even adding ‘behind all these schemes,’ the only one called a demon within the Heavenly Friends Alliance is Chung Myung.

“Then why do you think he provoked this situation?”

The answer came immediately, not from Baek Cheon but from the Five Swords gathered nearby.

“Because he has a nasty personality.”

“To watch us get beaten.”

“To torment us.”

“Because that’s just how he is.”

As Im So-Byeong flinched and tried to say something, Tang Pae and Namgung Do-Wei quietly added their words.

“Sounds about right.”

“Honestly, he doesn’t really need a reason.”

Im So-Byeong fell silent as if at a loss for words. He needed to overturn this with logic and rhetoric but couldn’t think of any.

“That’s... true, but... well... it could be... no, it’s natural. That’s just how he is.”

He had suffered too much to say otherwise. Too much.

“Anyway, that can’t be the whole reason, right?”

“If we force ourselves to think positively, maybe.”

“Then why did that demon... no, Taoist Chung Myung cause all this? Think! Think!”

“Well...”

Baek Cheon frowned slightly.

The reason they were made to fight each other? Honestly, he didn’t know. Was it to see how much resentment had built up?

“Then, on the contrary.”

“Yes?”

“What did you learn from all that fighting?”

While Baek Cheon was silent, searching for an answer, Jo Gul nearby spoke indifferently.

“What would I know? All I learned is how nasty those guys’ tempers are and how to deal with them better.”

“That’s it.”

“Yes?”

Expecting another scolding, Jo Gul was surprised to see Im So-Byeong nodding as if pleased with the answer.

“Exactly that!”

“...What?”

“How to better deal with each other?”

“...”

“That means, conversely, understanding the weaknesses of other sects and your own strengths. Using your strengths to strike the enemy’s weaknesses is the most basic principle of military strategy.”

Those who thought it was true nodded.

“What? Did I do well just now?”

Jo Gul’s desperate shout was completely ignored.

“And that means I also know what I can do. You’ve felt it while getting hit and hitting, haven’t you?”

“...”

“When someone like Mount Hua’s Sword Saint charges, what can you do? What will you do?”

Their expressions grew serious upon hearing that.

“Well...”

Seeing them troubled, Im So-Byeong smiled slightly behind his fan.

‘Think about it.’

You can’t just follow someone’s orders.

On the battlefield ahead, no one can give immediate orders. To survive, you must perfectly understand the abilities of those around you and quickly decide what you must do.

Not following orders, but thinking and acting on your own.

Before knowing the enemy, you must first know yourself, your sect, and the abilities and traits of those fighting alongside you.

“The Tang family...”

Before Tang Pae could speak, a Beast Palace member blurted out.

“We can’t confidently rush in and grab legs, but stalling time is what Beast Palace does best.”

“Oh?”

Im So-Byeong’s fan shook pleasantly.

Feeling all eyes on him, the Beast Palace member scratched his head awkwardly.

“We look rough and tough, so people often misunderstand, but our martial arts, mimicking animal movements, are efficient at dodging attacks. That’s how nature is.”

“Come to think of it...”

Ice Palace, who had faced Beast Palace the most, nodded in agreement.

“Good!”

Im So-Byeong snapped his fan shut.

It was good that someone spoke first, but it was also meaningful that Beast Palace, who usually kept a step back with an attitude of ‘let’s see how much you acknowledge us,’ spoke up first.

This meant they understood what he said.

“The Tang family can help too. Stopping those who charge in is what we do best. Beast Palace’s rampage makes attacking harder, but since we’ve interacted with them a few times, it shouldn’t be hard to understand.”

“But the Tang Family becomes vulnerable if approached too closely... going too close is...”

“That’s where us Ice Palace steps in. We’re confident in our defense!”

“No, using Ice Palace for defense is a waste. Ice Palace’s sword is better suited for offense. It’d be better if our Namgung blocks...”

“Mount Hua should attack rather than the Ice Palace...”

Once started, numerous opinions poured out from all directions. Not only those gathered in the center but also those standing behind began adding their words.

Amid the flood of words, sometimes fierce, sometimes gentle, Im So-Byeong lightly covered his mouth with his fan.

“It’s become simple.”

Isn’t it natural for people to become like this once a single goal is set?

Smiling slightly, Im So-Byeong folded his fan and spoke.

“If it comes to this, no matter who they are...”

“Then let’s use the Green Forest as a shield!”

“Honestly, they’re useless! A meat shield is perfect!”

“Yeah! Isn’t Green Forest tougher than Beast Palace? Just push them in first!”

Hearing that, Im So-Byeong, as if speechless, flared his eyes with anger.

“What? You want Evil Sect bastards to be meat shields? You filthy Justice faction bastards!”

Thus, the Heavenly Friends Alliance members argued, fought, and shouted through the night until dawn.

The next day.

“Oh my.”

Chung Myung energetically rolled his shoulders.

“Well then, shall we spar gracefully once again today?”

Chung Myung still had plenty of energy, but Tang Gunak and Meng So’s expressions were not as bright or vigorous as his.

“...Aren’t you tired?”

“Tired?”

Chung Myung glanced subtly at Tang Gunak and Meng So. Their faces showed clear signs of fatigue.

Of course, the moment they entered the training ground, their faces would turn stern like true warriors, but here, it seemed unnecessary to put on such an act and waste energy.

“Hey. What have you done to be so tired already?”

“Ugh...”

“Age is scary.”

Meng So and Tang Gunak shook their heads vigorously.

Putting aside Chung Myung’s martial power for a moment, his tireless stamina was something they simply could not match.

“After all, you’re just dealing with kids, so you’re not going to complain, are you?”

At Chung Myung’s teasing, the two shook their heads.

“Once is nothing... but when it happens two or three times, it’s not easy to get up in the morning.”

“People don’t fully recover just by cultivating qi and sleeping, you know?”

At that, Chung Myung clicked his tongue.

“When war starts, it’s not just three or four days, but sometimes a month or two without rest. Will you still say the same then?”

Chung Myung turned his head slightly.

“It seems the elders think similarly. When war breaks out, if you say, ‘I’m too old to keep fighting like you, so I’ll rest at the back,’ that’s when the table gets cleared. If you don’t want to become an old man in the back room, endure it no matter what.”

“Ughhh.”

“Ugh...”

Groans escaped from the elders one after another. Though they felt upset inside, it was hard to argue since nothing said was wrong.

“It shouldn’t be that hard in the first place, so why the fuss?”

At that, Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

‘Not that hard...’

In some ways, it’s true. Facing them isn’t that difficult, as long as you never show weakness and aren’t required to only subdue them.

‘Moreover... they are definitely improving with repetition.’

They were clearly learning how to face us, either physically or mentally.

“It’s not as easy as it sounds.”

At that moment, Meng So spoke.

“They might find it unfamiliar to face a group like us, but conversely, we also have no experience fighting so many enemies at once.”

Everyone nodded at those words.

“It’s true, who in the world has such experience? Even if sects train to face multiple opponents, it’s usually no more than five. We have to adapt too.”

Though Meng So unusually complained, Chung Myung cut him off sharply.

“Then we must adapt.”

His expression was cold, as if not even needing to listen to Meng So.

“If there’s something to adapt to, just adapt. You don’t really think the blades of the Demonic cult or Four Sects Alliance will avoid you and only target them, do you?”

“Well...”

As Meng So hesitated, Tang Gunak’s eyes gleamed strangely.

“...Do you think we’ll face such situations often in the future?”

“It’s not about how often. The important thing is that such situations will inevitably occur.”

Chung Myung added, as if that alone wasn’t enough.

“The weaker side must do something.”

“...”

“If you arrogantly sit back as usual, letting the juniors handle the fight while you aim for the enemy leaders, all the juniors will be killed before you even meet the enemy commanders.”

At those words, Tang Gunak, Meng So, and the elders’ faces stiffened slightly.

“Then those who can fill the lacking forces must do so. If a real war breaks out, you won’t have the luxury to leisurely assess the situation with your arms crossed. Never.”

Chung Myung’s eyes narrowed slightly.

“You have two choices: either watch your juniors die in front and excuse yourself by saying you’re waiting for the right moment, or fight at the very front, getting your hands dirty.”

“Hmm...”

“Which will you choose?”

As the elders tried to answer, Chung Myung waved his hand to stop them.

“Ah, enough of this. If I ask, everyone will say they’ll fight on the front lines. But in reality, few really do. Most get scared and retreat, and those eager ones don’t know their limits and end up dying first.”

“...Huh.”

“So take this chance to get used to what it means to face many opponents.”

Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

“I always feel that you have too many demands.”

“Everyone at Mount Hua does it.”

“...That’s why it’s so frustrating.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

Asking him to develop himself while training them is excessive. But wanting his disciples to grow, he can’t just back out.

“There’s nowhere else in the world that trains like this.”

“That’s why everyone is like this.”

“...”

“The problem isn’t just between sects. Even those who start as low disciples fail to understand their juniors once they rise. They vow not to be like that but later say, ‘You don’t understand,’ or ‘I understand now that I’ve been through it.’”

Tang Gunak cleared his throat, feeling a bit guilty.

“If it’s because they don’t know, just tell them. If you understand first, explain so others can too. Saying ‘You’ll understand later’ without doing that... isn’t that just the easy way out?”

“...That’s right.”

“So it’s not only them who must change.”

As Tang Gunak tried to speak, Meng So scratched his head.

“Enough nagging, let’s go quickly. It is easier to deal with them instead of getting scolded by you.”

“Yes!”

Chung Myung didn’t argue with that.

In fact, considering their rank and apparent age in the murim world, they aren’t people who should be scolded by Chung Myung. Objectively, it’s impressive these two listen to Chung Myung, just Mount Hua’s third class disciple, without resentment.

Knowing this, Chung Myung didn’t go overboard.

‘Ugh. There’s too much to worry about.’

In the past, just saying ‘What are you asking? Shut up and do as told!’ was enough to silence complaints before him. Now, he must speak gently to avoid hurting feelings.

‘Sahyung. This is how I live.’

‘Hey. That’s different from this.’

Chung Myung shook his head and headed to the training ground.

‘Will nothing change today as well?’

The fastest way to change a person is to pinpoint and tell them the problem. But sometimes, nothing changes that way.

This isn’t something he can teach; they must feel it themselves.

‘It won’t be that easy.’

Patience is needed. Like Chung Mun, who waited knowing Chung Myung wouldn’t change easily.

“Phew.”

Chung Myung stood there and took a deep breath.

He must not rush. “More haste, less speed” is one of Chung Myung’s least favorite sayings, but now he must engrave it in his heart.

If he drags them by the collar because they don’t change fast enough, chaos will ensue where he isn’t. If he can’t protect everyone, he must make everyone able to protect themselves.

No matter how arduous, if it must be done, it must be done.

“Alright.”

Chung Myung nodded and briskly entered the training ground around the corner.

“Now, are you ready to get hit enthusiastically today... huh?”

But he suddenly paused upon entering the training ground. Chung Myung’s head tilted slightly.

“Oh?”

The scene before his eyes was very different from usual.

Until now, each sect gathered separately waiting. But now, what he saw was a clear formation.

At the front stood the Beast Palace, behind them Namgung Family. At the rear, the Tang family was ready to intervene anytime, while Ice Palace and Mount Hua stood on the wings, swords drawn on both sides.

Though basic, it was clearly a sight that showed preparation.

“Hmm?”

“Ho...”

Meng So and Tang Gunak, who followed, also looked at the scene with interest.

Chung Myung lightly covered his mouth with his fist, not wanting to show his involuntary smile.

‘This is faster than I thought.’

He expected Baek Cheon, the Five Swords, and Im So-Byeong wouldn’t just take this lying down. But he thought it would take at least ten more days.

‘They say children grow faster than parents expect. I guess I’m human after all to feel this.’

Everyone was growing day by day. Watching closely made it feel slower.

Chung Myung’s gaze shifted to Baek Cheon, who wore a confident smile. Then he looked at Green Forest behind the Tang family, especially Im So-Byeong.

After confirming Im So-Byeong shrugged his shoulders, he composed his expression and lowered his hand.

“Seems like they’ve done something...”

What came from his mouth was not praise but a blatant sneer.

“Will that really change anything?”

“Who knows?”

Baek Cheon smirked, accepting Chung Myung’s provocation.

“I don’t know what will change. Maybe a sword will be stuck in that big mouth of yours.”

Namgung Do-Wei spoke politely.

“You might experience an unexpected defeat.”

Of course, the Tang Family hadn’t lost either.

“Even if everything falls apart, it’s hard to avoid an addiction. The Family lord is the same!”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung looked at everyone incredulously, and Im So-Byeong, fanning himself at the back, raised his voice.

“I always say this!”

His voice pierced everyone’s ears.

“Winning or losing is a later matter. First, just land a hit on that pathetic face!”

“Oh!”

“Damn! Then I can sleep well today!”

“Please! Please! Pleeease!”

Before the excitement cooled, Im So-Byeong pointed his fan forward at Chung Myung.

“If you’re confident, come at me?”

“Confidence?”

“If not why are you backing off so much? Are you scared?”

“Ah... really?”

Chung Myung chuckled and grabbed his sword.

Ssshhh.

His sword slowly slid out of its sheath. Seeing this, everyone swallowed hard.

“You seem quite confident...”

Tang Gunak and Meng So stood to Chung Myung’s sides. Behind them, the elders chuckled as they followed.

“You’ll just get hit a bit longer. I’ll show you the difference in level, you bastards!”

Im So-Byeong shouted loudly as Chung Myung charged with his sword raised.

“Trample him!”

“Kill him!”

“Justice will prevail! Villains!”

Everyone charged forward, their eyes blazing with killing intent.

Chung Myung lowered his stance and bowed his head slightly. A faint smile crossed his lips, hidden from their view.

His breath rose to his chin. The heavy breaths were almost spat out continuously, “huff, huff.” Yoon Jong gripped his sword tightly as if to break it.

‘That bastard is tired too. If only the sword lands just once by luck!’

Flames burst from his eyes.

“Uraaaaah! Dieee!”

He swung his sword with all his might and charged forward. But even while pouring out all his strength, Yoon Jong secretly knew.

That “just once by luck” never comes through when needed.

Whoosh!

Chung Myung’s sword, extending like a sharp spear, instantly deflected Yoon Jong’s incoming sword.

And then.

Thud!

The handle of the Dark Fragrant Plum Sword that flew in consecutively mercilessly pierced Yoon Jong’s chin.

“Ugh...”

The dimming sky filled Yoon Jong’s vision.

‘Damn it...’

Thud.

In the end, Yoon Jong, the last one remaining, collapsed to the ground like rotten straw. Chung Myung sheathed the dark hidden sword and clicked his tongue shortly.

“You’re messing around, I see.”

“...”

“What? Revenge? Revenge? Do these bastards think the murim world is easy? Do you think the result changes just by setting up a formation and charging with enthusiasm? If you think so, why even train!”

Chung Myung gave a snort and scoffed as he lectured the sprawled fighters.

“A hundred years away! You kids!”

“...”

“Ahh, you lot with no progress.”

Chung Myung turned sharply. The two sect leaders and elders, watching the scene with stern faces, left the training ground with him.

As they confidently turned the corner of the training hall connected to the training ground, they all suddenly sat down on the floor.

“Oh dear...”

“I really thought we were going to lose.”

Even Meng So and Tang Gunak leaned against the wall of the hall, struggling to keep their balance.

“...That was dangerous.”

“This time it was really dangerous.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

On the surface, it looked like an easy victory as usual, but this time it was really tough. Since their numbers were few, if they had lost momentum even once, they would have collapsed uncontrollably. If things had gone slightly differently, they themselves would have been lying there.

In that sense, they fought very well today, but...

“But, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Yes?”

Tang Gunak frowned slightly seeing Chung Myung still lively and responsive unlike them.

‘He really has no limits.’

Physical strength aside, fighting repeatedly like this would surely wear down one’s spirit. Where does such calm mental strength come from?

“In my opinion... today it might have been better...”

“If we had lost on purpose?”

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak quietly nodded.

Of course, no warrior likes to lose. In any situation, a warrior ultimately wants to win. So whether the opponent is his disciples or members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, Tang Gunak did not want to lose deliberately. But...

‘Given the situation.’

They probably made a big decision today. They put aside old grudges with those they had fought with and made a magnanimous decision. But since the result didn’t change, their morale could be broken.

Usually, in such times, people blame others. When things go well, everything is enjoyable, but when things don’t, people start blaming others first.

“I think so too. Even if losing on purpose is out of the question, we should have given the impression that fighting together had meaning.”

Meng So stroked his chin and supported Tang Gunak’s words.

Hearing that, Chung Myung smirked.

“I understand what you mean.”

“Hmm?”

“You seem to think your disciples are too weak. We may scold them, but actually, they don’t falter when put to the test.”

“...What do you mean by that?”

“See for yourself.”

Chung Myung gestured toward the training ground. Tang Gunak and Meng So quietly approached the corner of the hall and peeked out.

“The formation was wrong from the start! The Beast Palace can’t hold!”

“No, the Beast Palace did buy us some time, that part is true!”

“What good is buying time if you end up collapsing! To stop those monstrous fellows, the formation must be thickened! One of Mount Hua or the Ice Palace should give up offense and switch to defense!”

“Then the other side can attack comfortably without any sense of crisis! That only delays time but ultimately can’t win. Don’t you know the best defense is offense?”

“That’s just idealism!”

Those who were sprawled out had gathered in the center and were fiercely arguing. Meng So and Tang Gunak were exhaling heavily.

“Ah, damn it. My jaw still aches from the hit.”

“Maybe because I’ve been hit so much, now I can endure it a bit.”

“...That doesn’t sound like something you boast about.”

“Anyway!”

Jo Gul’s eyes lit up.

“Did you see Lord Tang panting today?”

“I did!”

“His breath was right up to his chin! I thought he was about to collapse! Hehehe!”

Tang Gunak got angry and tensed up, but Meng So gently grabbed his shoulder to calm him.

“What about the Beast Palace Lord? Wow! He’s so big that even moving his shoulder feels like an avalanche!”

This time, Tang Gunak quietly reached out and gently held Meng So’s trembling wrist.

“I didn’t do anything special, but today was really worth it.”

“At this rate, the day we land a real blow isn’t far off!”

“So let’s regroup! First, let’s think about what to do with those useless Green Forest guys!”

“Wait a minute. What about Mount Hua’s Sword Saint? Isn’t he the biggest problem?”

“Mount Hua folks will handle him somehow. Let’s not think about it.”

“No, why are you dumping that thing on us? What do you want us to do about that!”

Hearing the heated conversation, Tang Gunak and Meng So quietly stepped back. Their faces showed disbelief.

“Right?”

“Huh...”

Meng So seemed even more surprised than Tang Gunak.

“Those guys...”

Before they knew it, the Beast Palace members were mingling naturally with them. The Beast Palace is located in a very remote area even within Yunnan. Because of that, they rarely interact with ordinary people. Living only with beasts as companions, they traditionally don’t get along well with outsiders.

Those Beast Palace members were now talking casually not just with Yunnan natives but with Central Plains people, sitting half sprawled.

“I can’t understand it.”

It was enough to suspect Chung Myung had cast some magic.

“Usually, parents don’t really know their children well, and masters don’t really know their disciples well. They should know better, but strangely, they don’t.”

Tang Gunak and Meng So nodded while watching Chung Myung smile.

“So instead of worrying about them, you should worry about yourselves. You might end up embarrassed otherwise.”

“That won’t happen.”

“Nonsense!”

Chung Myung chuckled, turned around, and stretched.

“Well, that’s something we’ll have to see.”

Watching Chung Myung stroll away, Meng So and Tang Gunak shook their heads.

“Oh dear. Not a single part of my body is unscathed.”

“Gul... don’t take off your shirt in the diner.”

“We’re all men here, is there a problem?”

“There’s Soso too, isn’t there?”

“Ah, Soso is family.”

“Sahyung.”

“Huh?”

Tang Soso smiled brightly.

“I’ve never had a younger brother like a sahyung.”

“...”

“If I had a younger brother like a sahyung, I’d have buried him myself long ago. Be thankful you weren’t born into the Tang family.”

“...I’m always thankful for that.”

Sincerely.

At that moment, Baek Cheon stood up. Jo Gul sharply looked and asked.

“Huh? Sasuk, where are you going?”

Usually, Baek Cheon would wait for others to finish eating even if he finished first. So his sudden standing during the meal naturally aroused curiosity.

“It’s nothing. I have somewhere to stop by briefly. Eat your meal.”

“Yes, Sasuk.”

Baek Cheon quietly left the restaurant. The others paid little attention and soon busied themselves chatting among themselves.

Baek Cheon walked slowly along the river. His gaze casually rested on the flowing water.

“Phew.”

A sigh escaped his lips.

The reason he took time to come to the riverbank was because his recent frustration had grown considerably.

‘How did that guy do all this?’

Mount Hua had been led by Chung Myung. But recently, Chung Myung hasn’t spoken much about what happens inside Mount Hua.

Of course, Chung Myung has to handle the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance’s affairs, so he might not have the time to care about Mount Hua’s internal matters. That could be one way to think.

But Baek Cheon knows. Chung Myung is not the type to act like that. If necessary, he would reduce sleep to do what must be done.

For such a man to lose interest this much means...

‘It’s a silent pressure telling me to handle things myself now.’

Baek Cheon sighed deeply once again.

He doesn’t feel burdened by leading Mount Hua itself. Of course, he doesn’t think he can do as much as Chung Myung, but he doesn’t need to do as much as Chung Myung anyway.

Besides, he doesn’t lead Mount Hua alone. His disciples and sasuk help him. Moreover, even Monk Hae Yeon subtly watches over whether the workload on him is too much...

‘It’s almost embarrassing to be whining like this.’

So he certainly doesn’t feel burdened by being the great disciple of Mount Hua. It’s the work he was supposed to do originally. The task he temporarily left to Chung Myung is finally returning to its place.

That’s not the problem. There was another reason making Baek Cheon feel frustrated.

Baek Cheon quietly grasped the hilt of his sword and then let go weakly.

‘I’m stagnant.’

The problem is himself.

During the three years of seclusion, he grew remarkably. But since then, despite many experiences, he hasn’t felt his skills improving.

At first, he wasn’t too worried. This was a matter time would solve. But at some point, Baek Cheon realized.

‘There is no time.’

Watching Chung Myung and Jang Ilso face the high priest, Baek Cheon painfully realized that the time when they must fight for their lives would come sooner than expected.

And when that moment comes, Baek Cheon knew he would be able to do nothing.

“Phew.”

Even with a long exhale, his suffocating feelings did not fade.

Baek Cheon is strong. Objectively, he is definitely strong.

Namgung Do-Wei, who carries all of Namgung’s expectations, and Tang Pae, confirmed as the next head of the Tang family, honestly are no match for him.

Right now, he might even be able to subdue Jin Geum-Ryong within ten moves. Baek Cheon has grown into an unmatched powerhouse in his class.

But still, the reason he feels frustrated is that such skill is not particularly helpful in the massive battles where these huge factions clash.

‘What should I do?’

His skill is insufficient, and time is short. One day, he might have to watch his other disciples die right before his eyes.

And perhaps that time is closer than he thinks.

That worry and anxiety led Baek Cheon here. Maybe seeing the wide river would ease his mind a little.

But even looking at the flowing river did not ease his mind at all. Instead, it only made him feel more suffocated.

He sighed repeatedly and walked along the riverbank. How long he walked like that was uncertain.

“Huh?”

Baek Cheon’s steps suddenly stopped. Tilting his head, he listened carefully.

“What is this sound...?”

His eyes shone sharply.

“The sound of weapons clashing?”

At this late hour, in a place like this?

Baek Cheon instantly silenced his presence and quickly ran toward the source of the weapon sounds.

Baek Cheon quietly approached the dense bushes and lowered his stance a little. The sound of metal weapons clashing was now clearer and closer.

“Two?”

It didn’t sound like a fight involving many people...

Reducing his presence as much as possible, he cautiously moved forward. He approached the source of the sound closely and gently parted the thick bushes to the side.

‘Huh?’

At that moment, Baek Cheon involuntarily opened his mouth at the unexpected sight before him, just as a sound was about to come out.

“Shh.”

A voice came from right beside him. Startled, Baek Cheon tried to shout, but a hand quickly covered his mouth.

“Shh.”

“...”

Normally, someone like Baek Cheon wouldn’t easily allow another to approach his face, but this was an exception. The one covering his mouth was none other than Yu Yiseol.

“Quiet.”

Baek Cheon silently nodded, and Yu Yiseol removed her hand from his mouth. Though there were many questions about her appearance, that was not important now. He turned his gaze to the scene before him.

Clang clang clang!

Swords clashed against each other.

A young man dressed in pure white martial clothes was flung backward, rolling mercilessly.

“Ugh!”

A suppressed groan escaped the young man’s lips, but there was no time to feign pain. He reflexively twisted his body to the side.

Whoooosh!

A foot landed where the young man’s face had just been, leaving a clear footprint on the ground. It was obvious what would have happened if he hadn’t twisted away.

But before he could even relax, the one who stepped on the ground kicked the young man’s back as he turned his body.

Bang!

The young man flew like a frog kicked by a child and crashed into a large tree trunk.

Whoosh!

His body slid down slowly from where he had crashed. Watching indifferently, Chung Myung spoke in a low voice.

“Get up.”

“...”

“Why? Have you done enough now?”

Hearing those words, the tip of Seol So-Baek’s fingers twitched.

“Cough!”

He coughed dryly, blood mixed in. Seol So-Baek staggered as he got up. His strength seemed completely drained as he wobbled several times, but using his sword as a cane, he somehow managed to stand.

“Your reactions are slow.”

Chung Myung said, looking at him with indifferent eyes.

“Lacking strength is inevitable because your internal qi is insufficient. Being slow is also inevitable because your muscle strength is lacking. But slow reactions themselves are entirely your problem.”

Seol So-Baek’s hand gripping the sword trembled. It wasn’t from shock at Chung Myung’s words, but purely from exhaustion as his stamina was completely drained.

His condition was already a mess.

The pure white martial clothes, like the snow of the Northern Sea, were covered in dust and stained muddy brown, with patches of dried black blood. His originally pale face had lost even the faintest color, looking almost corpse-like.

“What?”

Chung Myung looked at Seol So-Baek coldly and asked.

“Shall we stop here?”

“Ah, no, Taoist!”

Seol So-Baek gripped his sword tightly with trembling hands.

“If you want to stop, you can. You’ve done enough.”

“No!”

Seol So-Baek gritted his teeth and said.

“Taoist, you said it yourself. Whether you’ve done enough or not is for yourself to decide!”

“...”

“I am not enough with this! So I must do more!”

Chung Myung snorted with a smile.

“Like that?”

“...”

“Let’s be honest. You don’t have to do this. No one will look down on you just because you’re weak. At least while I’m alive, no one in the Northern Sea will touch you.”

Seol So-Baek gritted his teeth.

“And objectively speaking, you’ve tried hard enough. No one can say you lived comfortably till now. So before you hurt yourself...”

“No!”

Seol So-Baek shouted, cutting off Chung Myung’s words. His voice was almost a scream. His bloodshot eyes stared straight at Chung Myung.

“I’ve done enough, I’ve worked hard!”

The sound of grinding teeth was clearly heard.

“What meaning does that have? The only thing that matters is that I’ve become so weak I can’t even participate in Ice Palace’s training!”

“Isn’t that natural? You started learning martial arts late in life, and you weren’t strong to begin with when you became the Palace lord.”

“Why is that so natural!”

Seol So-Baek shouted angrily.

“Is there anyone who understands that? How can the Ice Palace Lord be weak? How can I protect the Palace! And the people of the Northern Sea?”

“...”

“Being young doesn’t absolve responsibility! Saying you’ll be stronger later is no comfort to me! It means that I am weak now and can’t do what I must now!”

Seol So-Baek adjusted his grip on the sword stuck in the ground and pulled it out.

“I will become stronger. As the Ice Palace’s Lord, I will earn the right to lead Ice Palace like the other sect leaders. As soon as possible!”

“You’re such a stubborn fool...”

“Thanks to someone I learned this from.”

At that moment, Chung Myung rushed at Seol So-Baek and swung his sword. Seol So-Baek quickly raised his sword to block, but his body was inevitably knocked back.

He reacted in time but couldn’t withstand the force behind Chung Myung’s sword.

“Ugh!”

Seol So-Baek rolled violently and finally spat out bright red blood. Seeing this, Chung Myung approached with a nonchalant expression.

“Unfair, isn’t it?”

“...”

“You reacted clearly, but you were overpowered. This isn’t something you can fix. Then how are you supposed to win? Are you just supposed to keep losing?”

Seol So-Baek’s eyes wavered slightly. Those words perfectly reflected his feelings.

“But you know, the murim world is always like that. Unfair and endlessly unjust. Effort? There’s no guarantee you’ll be rewarded just because you try. But...”

Chung Myung smiled faintly.

“Still, what can you do? If all you can do is try, then you have to do that. Right?”

Seol So-Baek forced his trembling body to stand up.

“Cough!”

He coughed painfully but stubbornly regained his stance. Chung Myung calmly asked while watching.

“You know, right? Before this war breaks out, there’s almost no chance you’ll grow enough to carry your own weight. Even less chance you’ll fulfill your role as the Palace Lord.”

Seol So-Baek nodded.

“Will you still do it?”

“Yes!”

“Even if it’s meaningless?”

“Yes!”

There was not a trace of hesitation in Seol So-Baek’s answer. Seeing that intense gaze, a satisfied smile appeared on Chung Myung’s lips.

“Looks like you haven’t been hit enough. Then shall I keep beating you until you say you can’t go on?”

Chung Myung charged straight at Seol So-Baek. At that moment, Seol So-Baek gritted his teeth and stomped the ground to meet him.

Baek Cheon, watching the sparring—or rather, the one-sided beating disguised as sparring—turned his head to look at Yu Yiseol.

“Samae.”

“Yes.”

“...Since when have they been like that?”

“Since when?”

Yu Yiseol tilted her head slightly, as if she found the question hard to understand. But before Baek Cheon could elaborate, she answered.

“The first day? No, from the next day.”

“The next day?”

“From the day after the Ice Palace arrived.”

Baek Cheon flinched for a moment and looked at Seol So-Baek again.

“So today isn’t the first time?”

Then that Chung Myung guy has been beating them during the day and dragging Seol So-Baek out at night to train all this time?

No, no. That can’t be it.

“When did the Ice Palace arrive?”

Then the Ice Palace Lord has been enduring one-on-one beatings like that every night?

“Crazy...”

It’s not exactly a strange sight. He too has always been pushed around by Chung Myung as much as Seol So-Baek... no, even more. But aren’t he and Seol So-Baek on different levels?

Baek Cheon thought that such grueling training would be too much even for him to endure, let alone that young Palace Lord.

“But he’s enduring it?”

Baek Cheon was inwardly confused about how to interpret this scene. Then Yu Yiseol whispered in an indifferent voice.

“He’s growing.”

“...Huh?”

“Day by day. Astonishingly fast.”

Yu Yiseol is not someone who easily praises others. For her to say this means Seol So-Baek is truly growing rapidly.

‘Is it talent?’

No. It probably isn’t.

Of course, he might have some talent, but Yu Yiseol saying this means there’s something else besides talent. And there was no need to ponder what that was.

“Aaaaaah!”

Seol So-Baek charged at Chung Myung. He was in such a state that it was doubtful he could even reach him properly without collapsing.

Seeing that, Baek Cheon unconsciously clenched his fist.

‘That much...’

He had worked excessively hard. No one could deny that Baek Cheon had done his best.

But at this moment, Baek Cheon realized something himself.

‘I knew that too.’

He had worked hard enough, tried as much as he could, and inwardly thought it would be difficult to do more.

Watching Seol So-Baek dragging his feet but desperately swinging his sword one more time, Baek Cheon felt it. Somehow, he too had become someone who looked down on others from above.

‘I...’

Baek Cheon bit his lip slightly and turned his head to look at Yu Yiseol.

“Samae, why are you here?”

“...At first, it was by chance.”

Probably so.

Yu Yiseol had always disliked showing her personal training to others. It must have been very awkward to train in the manor where not only Mount Hua but other sects were present.

“No, that means... Samae has been doing that much and still doing personal training?”

Even Baek Cheon hadn’t been doing that recently.

“After that...”

Yu Yiseol started to say something but closed her mouth. It probably meant it was hard to explain the exact reason.

But Baek Cheon seemed to understand why Yu Yiseol wanted to watch this scene.

Bang!

Chung Myung kicked Seol So-Baek’s side and sent him flying. Then he spoke loudly toward the fallen Seol So-Baek.

“Leading someone isn’t that easy.”

“...”

“Standing in front is not hard. But leading someone is a completely different matter. You have to work harder than anyone, never fall behind, and have the conviction that you are moving in the right direction.”

“...Yes.”

“Conviction comes from doubt. Doubt that what you thought might be wrong. Someday, you will also face the moment when you fall into the illusion that the conclusion you reached after much thought has led you here and that you are still right. The moment you mistakenly think you just have to keep doing what you’ve been doing.”

Hearing that, Baek Cheon clenched his fist.

“Remember this.”

Chung Myung said coldly.

“When your position changes, what was right becomes wrong, and what was wrong becomes right. And no one knows when that will happen. It could be a year from now, tomorrow, or maybe even yesterday.”

“...”

“So if you don’t want to be wrong, you have to constantly think and doubt.”

Seol So-Baek nodded with difficulty.

“Leading is hard. But if you can do it...”

Chung Myung spun around once holding his sword.

“The Ice Palace will truly get a proper Palace Lord.”

“...Of course I will.”

“Anyone can say that. Come on.”

“Yes!”

Seol So-Baek gritted his teeth and charged at Chung Myung again.

Chung Myung lowered his sword and watched Seol So-Baek, who advanced with all his strength. Above them, a bright moon hung in the sky.

Baek Cheon unconsciously closed his eyes.

“It wasn’t stagnation. I was just standing still.”

He gripped the sword that felt like part of his own body. The touch of the sword, so familiar it had become comfortable, he felt again from the beginning, delving deeper inside himself.

“Is this the place?”

Hwang Jongi looked at the large manor visible before him.

“Yes, Lord. As far as I know, the people of Mount Hua are here.”

At the steward’s words, Hwang Jongi sighed deeply.

“Finally arrived. It was so far.”

“You didn’t have to come personally, Lord...”

“Don’t say such things.”

Hwang Jongi shook his head firmly.

“Mount Hua is both a friend and the biggest client of the Eunha Merchant Guild. Even if we have business and friendship, neglecting a client can turn close ties distant.”

“Ah...”

“And it’s not just Mount Hua here. The Northern Sea Ice Palace and Nanman Beast Palace, the Eunha Merchant Guild’s biggest clients, are also here. With the biggest clients gathered in one place, how could I not come? Even if it were the Western Regions, not the Yangtze River, I must come personally!”

“That’s a wise statement.”

The steward nodded repeatedly with an admiring expression.

After Hwang Mun-Yak’s death, Hwang Jongi struggled to fill the void, but recently he has led the guild so perfectly that the memory of Hwang Mun-Yak pales in comparison.

Everyone in the guild openly says that if things continue like this, the Eunha Merchant Guild will rise to become the world’s best guild under Hwang Jongi’s leadership.

“My father always said that trust and people are important to merchants. I am just following my late father’s words.”

Hwang Jongi looked at the manor with a firm face and smiled gently.

“And... personally, I wanted to see the people of Mount Hua. Whenever I am tired and weary, seeing them gives me strength. Honestly, there aren’t many as lively as they are.”

“Hahaha. They’re too lively, that’s the problem. With Mount Hua people leaving the mountain, not only Hwa-Um but also Shaanxi feels quieter.”

“That’s true.”

Hwang Jongi gave a bitter smile.

They say you don’t notice when someone arrives, but you notice when they leave.

‘That means Mount Hua has become so important in Shaanxi, all in just a few years.’

It’s tiring just to talk about it.

Before Mount Hua revived, the representative sect in Shaanxi was Southern Edge, and when the Southern Edge suddenly announced it would close its gates, many were worried.

But Mount Hua filled Southern Edge’s void so perfectly it overflowed. Now, its influence extends beyond Shaanxi to the entire Central Plains and even the Western Regions.

So, just Mount Hua’s brief absence causes Shaanxi to lose vitality.

“I’m also excited to meet the people of Mount Hua.”

“You’re not the only one feeling that way.”

Hwang Jongi chuckled and pointed to the manor’s gate.

“Let’s go in.”

“Yes, merchant lord.”

Hwang Jongi proceeded toward the manor.

“There doesn’t seem to be anyone guarding the gate?”

“Is it really necessary? This is where Mount Hua, Tang Family, and two of the Five Palaces reside. Anyone with a bit of sense wouldn’t dare act rashly.”

“That’s true.”

“Let’s go in. They aren’t so rigid as to demand formalities just because we opened the gate.”

“Yes.”

At Hwang Jongi’s words, the steward stepped forward and opened the gate. Hwang Jong-ui entered and smiled slightly.

‘Strangely, it feels like I’ve truly come to Mount Hua.’

The unique calmness of the Taoist path and the lively spirit of Chung Myung blend into Mount Hua’s atmosphere, felt even here. That’s how it is wherever Mount Hua is.

“Who is that person?”

Hwang Jongi saw the back of the person ahead and brightened. Meeting a familiar face right away lifted his spirits.

“Taoist Jo Gul! Isn’t that Taoist Jo Gul!”

All Mount Hua disciples wear the same martial uniform, making it hard to tell them apart from behind, but Jo Gul’s distinctive curly hair made him easy to recognize.

Hwang Jongi, delighted, approached Jo Gul with a smile.

“Long time no see, Taoist Jo Gul... oh my!”

When Jo Gul turned his head to look at Hwang Jongi, Hwang Jongi was shocked and stepped back.

“T-taoist Jo Gul?”

“Ah...”

Jo Gul recognized Hwang Jongi and bowed deeply.

“Young Lord... no, Merchant Lord, you have come.”

“A-are you ill?”

“...What?”

“Why does your face look like that? Your face?”

Jo Gul weakly brushed his face.

“...It’s a bit damaged.”

A bit damaged?

Hwang Jongi was at a loss for words as he looked at Jo Gul.

‘Even if you dug up a grave and took out a corpse, it wouldn’t look like this.’

Should he say he turned pale white or that he died blackened?

His bloodless pale skin was so rough it seemed bark would be softer, and the dark shadows under his eyes were as if drawn with ink.

Even deliberate makeup wouldn’t achieve this effect.

“W-what on earth happened?”

“What do you mean what happened...”

At that moment.

“Isn’t that Merchant lord?”

At the voice from beside him, Hwang Jongi quickly turned his head.

“...W-who?”

“Yes? Merchant Lord, it’s Yoon Jong.”

“T-taoist Y-Yoon Jong?”

Hwang Jongi blinked repeatedly.

Yoon Jong looked just as bad as Jo Gul, or in some ways worse.

“Did the Four Sects Alliance invade?”

Hearing that, Yoon Jong snorted in an uncharacteristic laugh.

“The Four Sects Alliance... If they were the enemy, it wouldn’t be so hard. We’d just... just beat them to death.”

“...”

“The problem is, there’s someone worse than the Four Sects Alliance here at Mount Hua.”

“That would be Taoist Chung Myung, right?”

Who else but Chung Myung could make someone look like this?

But Yoon Jong and Jo Gul shook their heads simultaneously at Hwang Jongi’s words.

“Chung Myung is better.”

“At least he was human.”

“...What?”

The two shook their heads without answering.

Just as Hwang Jongi, utterly confused, was about to ask again.

“You fools! I told you to come to the training ground, why are you wasting time there?”

At the angry voice, Yoon Jong and Jo Gul flinched and shrank their necks. Their faces twisted horribly.

Hwang Jongi also reflexively turned his head to look at the speaker.

“T-taoist Baek Cheon?”

“No. Isn’t that merchant lord?”

Baek Cheon bowed politely upon seeing Hwang Jongi.

“Long time no see.”

“Yes, yes... Taoist Baek Cheon.”

“Did you just arrive?”

“Yes, that’s right.”

“It must have been a long journey; you must be very tired. The sect leader will be glad to see you.”

Strangely, while others looked near death, Baek Cheon appeared much the same as Hwang Jongi remembered.

No, rather...

‘He even looks somewhat polished now.’

Is Baek Cheon the only one who hasn’t trained? No, that wouldn’t suit his personality.

“Let’s go. I’ll guide you to the sect leader.”

“Ah... yes.”

“Wait.”

Baek Cheon gave Hwang Jongi a gentle smile and subtly turned his head toward Yoon Jong and Jo Gul.

At the same time, his expression changed completely from when he looked at Hwang Jongi.

“Training time is over! What are you two doing here?”

“...We were just going.”

“Well... we stopped briefly to see Merchant lord.”

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul shrank like mice before a cat. Baek Cheon frowned deeply, clearly displeased.

“Hurry up. I’ll escort Merchant Lord.”

“Yes.”

“Yes!”

The two bowed to Baek Cheon and Hwang Jongi, then hurried toward the training ground.

“Seriously, why is he like that? We were cursing others together not long ago!”

“How would I know...”

“Chung Myung is better. Ugh! When a high-ranking person like Baek Cheon flips his eyes, there’s nothing you can do.”

“Shh. They’ll hear you.”

“They’re all dying. Why are we the only ones doing extra training? We’re already exhausted.”

“...Enough talk, let’s go quickly.”

“Ugh.”

Watching the two run toward the training ground, cold sweat broke out on Hwang Jongi’s neck.

Turning his head with a vague unease, Baek Cheon still wore a friendly smile.

“Let’s go.”

“...Yes.”

This man is scarier than I thought.

Walking through the manor guided by Baek Cheon, Hwang Jongi spoke softly.

“Are the members of other sects all here as well?”

“Yes. The afternoon training just ended, so everyone is either resting or gone to eat.”

“Ah.”

Hwang Jongi nodded.

“Mount Hua certainly has something special. Training more than other sects while all together isn’t easy.”

“Ah, that’s not the case.”

“What? Earlier you said other sects rest while only Mount Hua trains...”

“Ah, well.”

Baek Cheon scratched his head.

“They’re over there. You’ll understand when you see.”

“Yes?”

At Baek Cheon’s words, Hwang Jongi looked up at the scene ahead.

“...”

In the space between the large manors, dirt-covered people were literally sprawled out.

‘Is this a beggars’ den?’

No, rather than a beggars’ den... more like a group of defeated soldiers?

They looked like battered survivors barely alive, lying down.

Seeing those at the front with their heads buried in the dirt foaming at the mouth, Hwang Jongi was disgusted.

“N-no, they seem to be from the Beggars’ Union.”

“No.”

“What? Green clothes...”

Hwang Jongi closed his mouth.

Because they wore green clothes, he naturally thought they were Beggars’ Union members. If not, they wouldn’t throw away dignity and lie like that.

But he saw it.

The character ‘Tang’ engraved on their chests.

“They are from Tang family.”

“...”

Tang Family? These people?

Blinking, Hwang Jongi looked at those lying on the dirt, having thrown away all dignity.

‘No matter how I look, they look like beggars.’

Not just beggars, but those starving for at least two weeks.

Since when did Tang Family change their affiliation to the Beggars’ Union?

“W-water...”

“A-are you okay?”

“Ki-k... K-ki...”

“Crispy rice? You want crispy rice?”

“Kill...”

“...”

Seeing Hwang Jongi’s blank face, Baek Cheon glanced at him and said casually.

“Don’t worry about it. This happens every day.”

No, you crazy man!

This shouldn’t happen every day! How can this be a place where people live?

“Th-those in white... no, yellow clothes?”

“They are from the Namgung Family. Hmm, today the Namgung family members are in decent condition.”

“...”

Ah, those people?

The Blue Sky Namgung Family? The most upright prestigious family in the world?

After seeing the plague-like appearance of the village behind them, Hwang Jongi, shuddering, saw Im So-Byeong hanging on the wall like a dried squid and gave up thinking further.

‘Let’s not think about it strangely.’

Isn’t this always what happens where Chung Myung is?

It’s foolish to judge the place he is by common sense. So stay calm...

“Here we are. Hmm?”

Baek Cheon frowned upon arriving at the sect leader’s quarters.

“Excuse me, get up.”

He approached someone lying against the wall in front of the sect leader’s quarters, placing a hand on their shoulder, leaving Hwang Jongi behind.

‘That person... hmm? I feel like I’ve seen him somewhere.’

“Tang family lord, you can’t sleep here. Please get up...”

“Why is the Tang family Lord here!”

A loud shout finally escaped from Hwang Jongi’s mouth.

Why is the head of Tang Gunak sprawled like a local beggar in front of someone else’s quarters? Why!

“U-um. T-taoist Baek Cheon?”

“Yes, Family lord.”

“I was on my way to see the sect leader and stopped to rest, huh?”

“You should go to your quarters and rest a bit.”

“No. Hmm? It’s the Merchant Lord of the Eunha Merchant Guild.”

“H-have you been well, Family Lord?”

“Do I look well?”

“...”

Hearing that strangely sharp voice, Hwang Jongi bowed his head deeply.

“Please come in.”

“...”

Tang Gunak led the way into the quarters.

Seeing that, Hwang Jongi sighed as if the ground had collapsed beneath him.

‘I shouldn’t have come...’

Hwang Jongi felt deeply in his bones the reality of having come to Mount Hua.

What Hwang Jongi saw upon entering the room was a scene that was familiar, if familiar at all.

Hyun Jong sat in the center, flanked on both sides by the elders of Mount Hua. Indeed, it was a sight often seen even within Mount Hua.

The problem was...

“Who is that person...?”

Hwang Jongi blinked as he saw a huge man slumped against one wall like water-soaked cotton.

“I feel like I’ve seen him somewhere... Ah!”

Realizing that the large figure was the Beast Palace Lord he had seen during the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s initiation ceremony, Hwang Jongi flinched and shuddered.

What on earth had happened to drain all the energy from the body of that tower-like man? The way an ordinary person slumps and the way someone like him slumps felt completely different.

Moreover, as far as Hwang Jongi remembered, the Beast Palace Lord was a very polite person, contrary to his appearance...

‘He must have been through so much...’

It dawned on him anew that anyone entangled with Taoist Chung Myung and Mount Hua, be it the Beast Palace Lord or Tang Gunak, would be thrown into hell without exception.

‘And I would be no different.’

What? Missing Mount Hua?

Come to think of it, had there ever been a time as peaceful as the few months Mount Hua was absent from Shaanxi? They say when a person becomes addicted to alcohol, they think being drunk is natural, so what was I thinking missing them?

“Oh, Merchant Lord!”

Hyun Jong, Mount Hua’s sect leader, no, the leader of the demons, brightened upon seeing Hwang Jongi enter and stood up.

Without hesitation, Hyun Jong approached and firmly grasped Hwang Jongi’s hands, smiling brightly.

“Merchant Lord, how long has it been? Meeting you here in this distant place brings me boundless joy.”

“Yes. Well... yes. Sect Leader, I am also glad to see you...”

When a sect leader of Mount Hua shows such warmth, gratitude naturally arises, yet strangely, Hwang Jongi felt increasingly uneasy.

‘No, it’s not the sect leader’s fault.’

Holding Hyun Jong’s hand, Hwang Jongi quickly scanned the surroundings, searching for the one clearly responsible for all this trouble.

As always, the root of all evil was smiling cheerfully as if none of this had anything to do with him.

“You’re later than I expected.”

Though it was hardly a thing to say to someone who had rushed all the way from distant Shaanxi, Hwang Jongi didn’t take offense. He had been through too much to be swayed by such remarks.

“Have you been well?”

“What could possibly happen here? Of course, I’ve been well.”

“Ugh.”

“Sigh.”

As soon as Chung Myung answered, sighs and groans echoed from all around. Understanding the reason, Hwang Jongi just shook his head.

“It seems...”

Hwang Jongi, who had tried to joke seeing Chung Myung’s calm demeanor, suddenly tilted his head slightly.

“Hmm?”

“What’s wrong?”

After a brief hesitation, Hwang Jongi quietly approached Chung Myung.

“Would you stand up for a moment?”

“Sorry?”

Chung Myung tilted his head but stood up as asked. When Hwang Jongi stepped closer, Chung Myung backed away with a somewhat sulky expression.

“I don’t have this kind of preference...”

“You seem a bit taller.”

“Really?”

Chung Myung’s eyes widened at that.

Hwang Jongi looked Chung Myung up and down and nodded as if confirming it.

“Definitely.”

“Oh?”

Chung Myung stepped close to Hwang Jongi and measured their head heights with his hand. Voices around agreed.

“Yeah, I do seem a bit taller.”

“...Maybe you are still growing.”

“Ah! Heaven hasn’t abandoned me! I thought I wouldn’t grow anymore!”

This body was shorter than his previous one, which caused many inconveniences, but fortunately, it seemed he still had room to grow.

“No wonder my swordplay has felt so refreshing lately. Just wait, Heavenly Demon brat!”

“...I think you like the wrong part of it.”

“When you grow taller, your arms get longer, and longer arms are better for swordsmanship. Of course, too much isn’t good, but right now, I’m a bit stubby. Growing a bit more is better.”

Hwang Jongi shook his head. This person hadn’t changed since the first time he met him. It’s impressive how consistent he could be.

“Ah, right. Did you bring it?”

“Whose words were it, that could I not disobey? Of course, I brought it.”

“Ah, as expected, the only one I can trust is the merchant lord.”

“...I wish there was even a penny of sincerity in that statement.”

“Hehe. I always speak with perfect sincerity. So, where is it?”

“You seem to be happier about the item than about seeing me.”

“No way. I just want to be sure, that’s all.”

Hwang Jongi chuckled at that.

“It’s something hard to bring inside, so I left it by the door.”

“Oh!”

No sooner had he finished speaking than Chung Myung’s eyes lit up and he dashed to the door, flinging it wide open.

Those curious about what had been brought peered over to see the object placed by the door.

“A cauldron?”

“What is that?”

It was a dark-colored cauldron placed by the door. Those who saw it were puzzled. Only the Mount Hua sect members in the room knew what it was.

“It’s a Cold Steel cauldron.”

“A Cold Steel cauldron? Did you say Cold Steel?”

“It’s a cauldron made from eternal Cold Steel. Now that I think about it, we had stored it in the Merchant Guild.”

At that, the eyes of those not from Mount Hua widened.

Why on earth would someone make a cauldron out of such precious eternal Cold Steel? It’s not like money grows on trees.

“No, this is madness!”

Especially Tang Gunak rushed out and examined the cauldron, then muttered in a daze.

“It’s really eternal hancheol... Last time they made all their swords from hancheol... Does Mount Hua have a hancheol vein or something? Now they’re even making cauldrons...”

Watching Tang Gunak in a daze, Hyun Jong whispered to Chung Myung.

“Did Lord Tang not know about this?”

“Come to think of it, I don’t think I ever told him. There was no particular reason to.”

“That’s true.”

“But why are you so late?”

Hwang Jongi gave a bitter smile at Chung Myung’s question.

“It’s no ordinary item. If Mount Hua carries it, no problem, but if we get robbed while moving it, that would be a big issue. It took some time to arrange guards.”

“Ah... I didn’t think of that. If I had, I would have sent some idle bandits to fetch it.”

“...”

Hwang Jongi gave up thinking. The events in the Heavenly Friends Alliance had long since exceeded his scope of understanding.

“Anyway, thank you for your hard work.”

“Ugh.”

At that moment, Meng So, staggering to his feet, looked at the cauldron outside the door and chuckled bitterly.

“Why did you ask to bring that?”

“It has its use.”

“Use?”

“The kids have been looking quite weak lately.”

“Who’s to blame for that!”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“They say you give a disease and then the cure. Since we gave them the disease, now we must give the medicine.”

“...That’s not the right phrase to use here.”

“Come on. I know the Central Plains language better than the Palace Lord. This is exactly the phrase to use here.”

The Beast Palace Lord’s huge head drooped. Everyone felt pity but no one dared defend him. They all knew it was pointless to argue with Chung Myung.

“But medicine?”

“We had the ingredients but forgot to bring the cauldron. Now that it’s here, we can give the kids some tonic.”

At that, Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung admiringly.

Indeed, he had been worried lately that the sect disciples were struggling too much and wondered if it was time to intervene again.

“I see. That’s a good idea.”

Just as Hyun Jong was about to nod, Meng So, looking flustered, hurriedly asked.

“W-wait! Who are you giving what to?”

“Medicine for the kids.”

“To whom?”

“I said to the kids! Palace Lord, your energy must be really low if you can’t understand that.”

Meng So stared at Chung Myung incredulously.

“...By kids, do you mean the disciples of Mount Hua?”

“No, I’ll give it to all of them. Everyone here.”

At that, Meng So’s gaze wavered.

“Even the Beast Palace?”

“Of course. The most important ingredients were brought by the Beast Palace and the Ice Palace; you can’t leave them out. One must have a conscience.”

Meng So closed his mouth tightly. After a long moment of contemplation, he spoke in a heavy voice.

“...It doesn’t make sense.”

“What?”

“The value of the ingredients and the finished spiritual pill can’t even be compared. No matter that we brought the ingredients, no one would think receiving the spiritual pill in return is a fair trade.”

“...”

“But you’re saying you’ll give it away without any compensation?”

This was too much. Though the Heavenly Friends Alliance had declared it would treat sect disciples and affiliated sects without discrimination, no one truly believed it was possible.

Even Meng So, one of the core members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, did not truly wish for that. Yet now, Chung Myung seemed intent on actually doing the impossible.

At Meng So’s flustered voice, Chung Myung chuckled.

“No compensation? What are you talking about?”

“...Huh?”

An evil smile spread across Chung Myung’s face.

“Even when you use oxen, you have to feed them well to work well, and when you ride horses, you have to feed them carrots so they obey, right?”

“...”

“If even beasts need that, shouldn’t people be fed even better so they don’t work like oxen or horses?”

“...”

“These guys have been complaining about being tired lately, so let’s stuff their mouths with the spiritual pill and see if they still complain. If they have even a shred of conscience, they’ll obey without a word. Hehehe!”

Meng So trembled. Seeing his expression, Chung Myung nodded knowingly.

“Don’t worry. The Palace Lords and elders will get a pill each as well.”

“W-we too?”

“You’ve been struggling dealing with the kids lately. How can you handle them if they run wild after taking the spiritual pills? You’re getting older and your qi is waning.”

“...”

“The elders should eat well too, so it’s easier to handle the oxen or horses... no, the lively young ones. It’s all thought through.”

...Such kind words. How could giving away precious spiritual pill for free be a bad thing?

But everyone here painfully realized that depending on who says it and in what situation, it could be a bad thing.

“Eat well and grow strong. Once they take this pill, they won’t even dare complain about being tired for months!”

As Chung Myung’s eyes gleamed, Meng So quietly backed away.

“I-I think I don’t need to take it...”

“Take it.”

“No, really, I’m fine...”

“Take it.”

“...Yes.”

A pleased and proud smile spread across Chung Myung’s lips as he looked at the cauldron outside the door.

“Once they finish the Heavenly Violet Pill, we can push them harder than now. I’ve been frustrated anyway; this came at just the right time. Hehehe.”

Hearing that, everyone’s faces darkened. A black storm cloud was gathering over the pavilion where the Heavenly Friends Alliance stayed.

“Did you see it yesterday?”

“What?”

“You know, that... the manor where the Heavenly Friends Alliance members reside.”

“Ah. That. Why bring it up all of a sudden? That place has long become a famous landmark in the river mouth.”

The listener chuckled dryly. As he said, the manor where Mount Hua resides had already become very famous in the river mouth.

“Don’t even mention it. This time, that... Mr. Jang has fallen ill and is bedridden.”

“Again?”

“What do you mean again? I told that gentleman to hire more people earlier, but he didn’t listen.”

“Didn’t listen? Man, he did hire people. But those he hired couldn’t last three days and ran away!”

“That’s true.”

“Oh dear. That gentleman is down again. He’s raking in money like with a rake.”

“Hey, is money the problem? People have to live first.”

“Heh heh heh. I’d be fine dying if I could earn that much.”

The Mr. Jang they spoke of is none other than the operator of the largest food supply store in the river mouth of the Yangtze. Mount Hua, located in the manor, requested him to supply ingredients, bringing him both fortune and misfortune.

“No, what kind of food do they eat so much of?”

“Aren’t they warriors who use their bodies? Naturally, they eat more than ordinary people.”

“Even so, the amount they eat could feed all of this land and still have leftovers. And it doesn’t seem like there are that many people...”

“Who knows? How could we understand how the warriors of the murim world live?”

There was no need to do anything publicly. The enormous amounts of meat and grain entering the manor were enough for the Mount Hua manor to become a famous landmark in the river mouth.

“And Mr. Yu is also bedridden.”

“Why Mr. Yu? He isn’t related to food supplies, is he?”

“Hey, you keep saying nonsense. What does Mr. Yu do? Isn’t it cutting grass?”

“Right?”

“I heard that the manor is overflowing with beasts from Yunnan, and the amount of grass they consume is no joke.”

“Ah, so that’s why Mr. Yu recruited a large number of workers last time... Wait a minute? But he must have hired at least twenty then. And now he’s bedridden?”

“What do you mean twenty? It’s been over thirty for a while. Half of them are now bedridden. They cut grass day and night, and the mountains around the river mouth are about to become bare hills.”
