“A fundamental question? What do you mean?”

“Well... you seem to be saying it’s important to protect those small and medium sects.”

“That’s right.”

“Do we have to care about their lives too?”

“...”

“Frankly, what have they done?”

“...”

“...”

Everyone was speechless and looked at Chung Myung. But he tilted his head as if he truly didn’t understand.

“Did I say something wrong?”

“Well...”

At that moment, those stunned stared blankly at Chung Myung, then all closed their eyes tightly. Even Hyun Jong covered his face with both hands, unable to say anything.

How... how did such a person come out under Hyun Jong? And how is everyone here taking orders from such a person?

Chung Myung wasn’t completely oblivious; he looked around and subtly changed the subject.

“Well, if it’s absolutely necessary, I won’t oppose...”

“Excuse me, Namgung’s young lord.”

At that moment, Jo Gul quietly spoke up.

“Yes? Yes, Taoist Jo Gul.”

“If we follow the young lord’s words, that guy will also take a reasonably high position, right?”

Namgung Do-Wei turned his head to look at Chung Myung and thought for a moment.

Chung Myung huh, Chung Myung...

“I... suppose so?”

No matter how much he thought, there was no way to exclude that person. If it’s about efficiency, giving him a position like a general so that everyone except the sect leaders obeys his orders...

As he continued thinking, Namgung Do-Wei suddenly realized the seriousness of the situation and froze. His face instantly turned pale.

‘He’s even going to wear a hat for the nonsense he does now?’

That person?

Jo Gul hurriedly raised his hand.

“I, I oppose...!”

“Oh, really?”

But at that moment, Chung Myung didn’t miss the flow and suddenly interrupted.

“So if you don’t listen to me, does that make you a rebel?”

“...W-wait a moment. I...”

“Then I agree.”

Chung Myung’s mouth curled into a sly smile.

“Let’s create that position or whatever it is.”

At that moment, everyone’s sharp gaze pierced Namgung Do-Wei.

Only then did Namgung Do-Wei realize what he had done. His soul began to leave his body.

When I barely came to my senses, the first thought that crossed my mind was just one.

‘I have to, I must manage this!’

If I can’t somehow manage this situation, the moment I leave this room, I might be trampled to death under the feet of those glaring at me with ghostly eyes.

Then a tombstone engraved with ‘Namgung father and son went side by side into the Yangtze River’ would be erected.

Everyone would gossip. They’d say the father bravely fought and died, but the son foolishly talked nonsense and was punished to death.

‘Ah, no.’

Among the many ends Namgung Do-Wei had imagined for himself, there was no such scenario. Trembling with extreme anxiety, he desperately spoke up.

“F-First, it might be better to postpone this agenda a bit...”

“Why? It looks good. We can do it right away. What’s the big deal?”

“N-No, that’s not the case!”

Namgung Do-Wei’s mind began spinning more fiercely than ever before.

“I-It is truly important to create the right positions and appoint the right people. This is not something to be decided hastily in a place like this!”

“Hmm?”

“Isn’t it said that personnel is everything? Dynasties that failed to manage personnel properly always fell! So you must take this seriously!”

“...That much importance for this?”

“Of course it is!”

“Hmmm.”

Chung Myung smacked his lips as if something was unsatisfactory.

“But wouldn’t it be okay to just decide a few important positions first and fill the minor ones later?”

“P-People’s minds don’t work that way. If you decide the important positions first, selecting the subsequent ones tends to become a bit lazy!”

“...”

“Please understand that all of this comes from my heart thinking of the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

“Well, if you put it that way...”

“Y-Yeah. Chung Myung, I don’t think this is something to decide right here.”

“That’s right, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. We’ll think deeply about this with the elders.”

When Hyun Jong and Tang Gunak sided with Namgung Do-Wei, Chung Myung smacked his lips and subtly stepped back.

“Well, if that’s how it is...”

“Haha. Yeah, yeah. This isn’t such a simple matter, after all.”

At that moment, Chung Myung glanced at Namgung Do-Wei.

“By the way...”

“Yes?”

“Namgung young lord, your tongue has become quite skillful.”

“...”

“I don’t think it was like that before. Maybe it’s because you’ve been hanging out with that Evil Sect guy lately?”

“...”

“You are learning good things. Very good.”

Feeling wronged, Namgung Do-Wei grimaced and opened his mouth slightly. No matter what, comparing him to the Evil Sect leader was too much!

“I’ll let it slide this once.”

Chung Myung chuckled and turned his head to look at everyone.

“Then, what about the other agenda?”

“Hmm. Agenda, huh...”

Tang Gunak cleared his throat slightly. Though he had experienced this many times, he was still not used to everyone exchanging opinions equally. But at times like this, he had to say something.

“Listen, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Yes, family lord.”

“A war will break out, right?”

Chung Myung’s gaze at Tang Gunak darkened. It meant, ‘Why bring this up now?’

“Then do you intend to maintain this Yangtze front until the war breaks out?”

“That’s not for us to decide.”

“Huh?”

“The Nine Great Sects decide that. Those bastards haven’t withdrawn yet, so it wouldn’t be right for us to leave first.”

“That’s certainly true.”

Tang Gunak frowned slightly.

‘What on earth is Beop Jeong thinking?’

The Heavenly Friends Alliance also somewhat accidentally settled here, but in fact, the Nine Great Sects including Shaolin and the Five Great Families settled on the Yangtze more abruptly.

More precisely, among the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families, some sects allied with Shaolin.

Hearing this, Baek Cheon scratched his head.

“Come to think of it, we’ve been so busy with immediate matters that we haven’t paid attention to them, but what exactly are they doing?”

“Huh?”

“Right? We train here, and since it’s unclear where the Palaces would join us, so we naturally settled here... but the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families aren’t in that situation.”

“That’s true.”

“They seem like they could withdraw anytime...”

At first, the Nine Great Sects couldn’t easily leave the Yangtze because they didn’t know when the Evil Sect would cross the river. But now?

“...They must know that the Evil Sect needs internal reorganization.”

“That’s underestimating the Nine Great Sects. Of course, they know it.”

They couldn’t understand at all. If the Evil Sect isn’t advancing north, the Nine Great Sects don’t need to guard the Yangtze basin. So why are they still stationed there?

“Why think of it so complicatedly?”

“Hmm?”

Everyone’s gaze fixed on Chung Myung’s face. His expression was utterly indifferent.

“I don’t think they’re thinking that deeply. Just think from their perspective.”

“...What do you mean by that?”

“If you were that bald leader, what would you do? You rushed here desperately, but nothing happened. You tried to take advantage of the Demonic Cult’s rise, but some guys went and solved everything.”

Putting themselves in his shoes, they thought it must be quite frustrating.

“We don’t care about such things, but that big bald guy cares a lot.”

“You’re talking about face and justification.”

“Yes, exactly.”

Chung Myung chuckled and continued.

“I don’t know for sure, but in that situation, they probably didn’t want to leave the Yangtze before us. At least they want the justification that they guarded the Yangtze until the very end. At first, they probably thought just one thing: watch carefully, and when we disband and return to our main sects, they would withdraw too.”

“...Ah.”

“Yes. But we didn’t go home.”

“...”

“In that situation, if we ask them why they don’t go home even now, they’d be so frustrated they’d die.”

Tang Gunak gave a hollow laugh.

“What you say isn’t entirely wrong, but leaving the sect’s base and spending time here isn’t as easy as it sounds. If there were no sects like Mount Hua that influence Southern Edge into closed gate, and Shaanxi, it wouldn’t be a problem, but for other sects, even a brief absence could cause fatal problems.”

“Hmm?”

“Of course, the face and justification you mentioned do have some influence, but thinking they stay there only for that reason seems a bit much. It can’t be just that.”

Just as Tang Gunak spoke with conviction, Im So-Byeong suddenly interrupted.

“Why do you only think halfway to the point?”

Tang Gunak’s gaze turned to him. Im So-Byeong leaned against the wall with a languid face and spoke.

“Sun Tzu said ‘Know the enemy and know yourself, and you will never be defeated.’ But our noble Justice faction members all know the enemy but don’t know themselves.”

“What do you mean by that?”

Tang Gunak asked, and Im So-Byeong chuckled.

“Think about it, think. They’ve been watching what we’ve been doing all this time, haven’t they?”

“I suppose so?”

This manor’s structure doesn’t allow internal matters to be hidden from outsiders. Moreover, many people in charge of the manor’s affairs come and go, so they could easily obtain information without mobilizing the Beggars’ Union.

And in fact, in some ways, they would be more curious about the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s movements than the Evil Sect’s, so naturally, they paid attention.

“Then what do you think they’re thinking?”

“What do you mean by what they’re thinking?”

“I’m telling you to think about what they’ve seen and heard all this time.”

“What they’ve seen and heard, well...”

At that moment, Baek Cheon spoke.

“Mount Hua and the Tang Family have been fighting all day long.”

Yoon Jong supported his words.

“The Tang Family’s lord got angry and beat the elders and disciples like catching rats.”

Tang Pae sneaked in.

“Suddenly, the Beast Palace from Yunnan joined in and is fighting with other sects there?”

Namgung Do-Wei continued, sweating coldly.

“C-Could it be that rumors have spread that the Namgung Family is fighting with the Green Forest?”

Just as Tang Gunak’s expression twisted, Jo Gul, with his hands clasped behind his head, smiled and spoke.

“And as the final blow, they probably heard that Chung Myung, who saw that mess, went half crazy.”

“...Huh?”

“Do you understand?”

Im So-Byeong hammered the point as if to say, ‘Look at this.’

“Right now, the ones who want to ask ‘What the hell are you doing here?’ are probably not us, but them.”

Tang Gunak could neither laugh nor cry now.

“Abbot.”

“...”

“How long do you intend to stay here?”

“...”

“Surely you don’t intend to bury your bones here?”

The abbot closed his eyes and remained silent. Jong Ri-Hyung didn’t give up and pressed him again.

“Abbot, you know as well. The Four Sects Alliance can’t advance north for a while. The Pirates occupying Plum Blossom Island don’t have the capacity to cross the Yangtze. The Pirates, whose leader is injured, can’t act alone without the Four Sects Alliance’s support.”

“...”

“Then why do you keep staying here? How many days have you been wasting time endlessly?”

Jong Ri-Hyung couldn’t contain his frustration and pounded his chest.

“The complaints among the disciples are indescribable. I understand you, but you must also understand their feelings, stuck in this distant place after hastily leaving the main sect! The Peng Family side has already been talking about leaving here alone quite often!”

“...”

“Abbot! Please say something!”

At that moment, the abbot slowly opened his closed eyes. But that was all; he just looked at Jong Ri-Hyung with deeply sunken eyes. Still no words.

“...abbot.”

At that response, Jong Ri-Hyung seemed to make up his mind and spoke. His voice was lower than usual.

“I didn’t want to say this... but among the kids, there’s talk that the abbot got scared. They say once bitten by a snake, twice shy. Do you understand what I mean?”

“...You said ‘twice shy’?”

“Yes, abbot! That’s how much right now...”

“What’s the problem with that?”

“...Huh?”

Flustered, Jong Ri-Hyung opened his eyes wide and looked at the abbot. The abbot remained calm.

“What’s wrong with someone bitten by a snake being startled by a pot lid? Pretending nothing happened and getting bitten again is more foolish.”

The abbot slightly gritted his teeth.

“Even if I’m called foolish and frustrating, I can’t let them disgrace me again. Am I wrong for that?”

“A-abbot.”

The abbot’s eyes were clearly on Jong Ri-Hyung sitting opposite him. But what he was truly seeing was the image of Chung Myung beyond Jong Ri-Hyung.

’ Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.’

The abbot, Beop Jeong, exhaled deeply through his nose.

‘What scheme is he up to now?’

Chung Myung’s phantom chuckled. Beop Jeong unconsciously clenched his fist tightly.

“...Abbot.”

Beop Jeong tightly closed his eyes.

‘Is it Heart Demon?’

Lately, whenever he thought of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, his heart often churned. Even though he knew he shouldn’t feel that way.

“Amitabha.”

He recited the Buddhist chant out of habit to calm his mind as much as possible. After regaining composure, he opened his eyes and met the worried gaze of Jong Ri-Hyung.

“I understand what the sect leader is worried about.”

“...”

“I fully understand that leaving the main sect unattended for a long time is not good. I also understand that you are anxious because it seems like time is being wasted here without any significant results.”

“That’s not necessarily the case, though...”

Jong Ri-Hyung, feeling awkward, subtly shifted his position. Beop Jeong spoke.

“But in the current situation, it is difficult for us to withdraw from here first.”

“Abbot, I understand your feelings, but staying like this won’t gain us anything either.”

“...”

“To be frank, we have already lost the people’s support at the Yangtze River.”

Jong Ri-Hyung sighed and continued.

“It’s not just about the main sect. The attitude of the locals toward us and toward the Heavenly Friends Alliance is noticeably different. As a result, the morale of the young disciples is steadily declining.”

“...Amitabha.”

“I’m worried that this won’t just be a waste of time, but that we might lose something we must not lose. Lost time and benefits can be somehow recovered, but once pride is broken, it is not easily restored, as you well know, abbot.”

Jong Ri-Hyung’s point was valid. He too was a sect leader of a martial arts sect. He was not someone who could not discern right from wrong.

From his perspective, what the abbot was doing now was nothing more than stubbornness.

“Abbot, they say victory and defeat are the way of soldiers. Even if we lose this time, isn’t it the one who wins in the end who truly wins?”

“...That’s right.”

“This place is already a battlefield we have lost. A great general does not cling to defeat. Rather, we should use that time to establish a new battlefield and focus our energy on reversing the defeat.”

The abbot’s lips twitched slightly. He did not like the word ‘defeat’ that Jong Ri-Hyung used.

Nothing pleased him.

He disliked the word defeat, disliked that the defeated opponent was not even the Four Sects Alliance but the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Moreover, he disliked hearing the word defeat when in reality they had not even exchanged a single punch with anyone.

But the most uncomfortable and frustrating part was that even hearing those words, he did not dare to refute them.

A war not lost, yet lost.

That was how Beop Jeong viewed this Yangtze River Battle.

“Several sects that have received your position have already expressed their willingness to support.”

“That is correct.”

“They too must have thought they could not just stand by in this situation. But abbot, where would you have them come? To this Yangtze River? Would you have them come here when no war is even breaking out?”

“...”

“Do you intend, as the Shaolin’s abbot, to receive guests here like a guesthouse? What would they think if they saw you here? Would they truly admire you, the abbot, who burns yourself to keep the Four Sects Alliance in check?”

Beop Jeong’s lips twitched again, but Jong Ri-Hyung did not stop speaking despite that reaction.

“A person’s authority comes from their position. Position can mean rank and status, but literally also means the place where one is. An emperor sitting on the throne in the imperial palace and an emperor sitting in a tent on the field cannot look the same, can they?”

He was not wrong.

“The place where you should welcome them is not here. The place you should be is none other than Shaolin. Am I wrong?”

A rare sigh escaped from Beop Jeong’s mouth.

Everything was correct. All correct. But the problem was that Beop Jeong was not here wasting time because he was unaware of all this.

‘A defeated battlefield...’

Perhaps those words were right. There is nothing more foolish than clinging to a battlefield already lost. Trying to regain momentum in a lost battlefield only increases damage.

But...

“It isn’t that I do not understand what the sect leader is saying. However...”

Seeing Beop Jeong hesitate slightly, Jong Ri-Hyung tactfully asked first.

“Is it because of the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“...”

“Abbot... I understand that you are concerned about them. But since the front line has already become fixed, what can they do?”

Jong Ri-Hyung quickly continued, sounding frustrated.

“I also do not know why they continue to stay here. But why should that be a reason for us to stay together? If they stay here to block the Four Sects Alliance and voluntarily become a shield, let them do so. How is that our loss? It is a gain, not a loss. Right now, abbot, you are ignoring that simple...”

Beop Jeong’s eyebrows twitched momentarily.

“You said ‘simple’?”

At his icy voice, Jong Ri-Hyung tightly closed his mouth.

Beop Jeong, realizing he had overreacted, quietly closed his eyes and recited the Buddhist chant. Then, with a softer voice free of harshness, he spoke slowly again.

“Sect leader, then I will ask. Do you think I have made any decisive mistakes since arriving here at the Yangtze River?”

“That is...”

Jong Ri-hyung could not answer easily. His first thought was ‘yes.’ Looking back, everything Beop Jeong did after getting involved with the Yangtze River was a mistake.

But Jong Ri-hyung was not someone who thought so simply.

‘Did the abbot really make a bad move?’

That was just hindsight. At the time, Beop Jeong made the best judgment. In fact, when others were swayed by emotions, he was the only one who remained calm.

The problem was that all those calm and sensible judgments turned out to be bad moves. No, the Heavenly Friends Alliance forcibly turned all those judgments into bad moves.

Was it really the right decision to advance to Plum Blossom Island where the enemy was entrenched and waiting in ambush? At the time, even he was disgusted by Beop Jeong’s coldness, but any three-year-old would know what would have happened if they had advanced to Plum Blossom Island.

Wasn’t it Jong Ri-Hyung himself who said one should not cling to a lost war? It was just that Mount Hua overturned that already finished battle, making Shaolin and Kongtong mere bystanders.

‘The Demonic Cult was the same.’

Is it really a sensible and normal decision to head to Hangzhou, which is essentially the stronghold of the Four Sects Alliance and the Black Ghost Fortress, to stop the demonic cult?

It was also Jong Ri-Hyung who said a great general must choose a winning battlefield. Even he thought Hangzhou was not a battlefield they could fight on. That judgment was absolutely correct.

If Mount Hua had not alone invaded the south and beheaded the high priest, reducing Shaolin and Kongtong to cowardly cowards.

“Abbot...”

“No matter how much I think about it, I don’t know. What mistake did I make? Ten times, a hundred times, a thousand times I reconsider, and the judgment I should have made is clear.”

Jong Ri-Hyung nodded. Even he could not deny this.

“But what is the cost of that correct judgment? The momentum of the Heavenly Friends Alliance has risen beyond comparison, and they have taken all the credit for the events at the Yangtze River. Everyone in the world points fingers at Shaolin and praises Mount Hua. Even!”

Crunch.

Beop Jeong grabbed the wooden table in front of him. His fingertips dug into the wood.

“Even the Four Sects Alliance is being said to be better than Shaolin, who stood idly by, because they contributed to stopping the Demonic Cult.”

“...”

“It’s a lamentable matter.”

Beop Jeong let out a hollow laugh.

“By the natural order, they should have already been ruined. But did that happen? They won on the battlefield they should have lost and gained benefits where they should have lost. In ways unimaginable.”

“...”

“I ask again, sect leader. Is it really wrong for someone who has been bitten by a snake to be wary of it? Is that really such a foolish thing?”

“Abbot...”

“No. In my opinion, nothing is more foolish than repeating the same mistakes after suffering enough. If you cannot understand with your head, at least do not repeat the same mistake.”

“...So you remain here because you cannot let your guard down, not knowing what the Heavenly Friends Alliance might do?”

“...”

“Do you think there is a reason they do not leave this Yangtze River?”

Beop Jeong removed his hand from the table without answering. His palm was soaked with sweat. Looking at his own palm, he finally let out a bitter laugh.

“I understand that I may seem frustrating.”

Jong Ri-Hyung could not bring himself to say no.

“Amitabha. But if we make one more mistake, we might be driven to an irreversible situation. We must prevent that, mustn’t we?”

Jong Ri-Hyung closed his eyes tightly and spoke. He realized that no matter what he said here, he could not change Beop Jeong’s mind.

“Since you say so, abbot... I will no longer try to persuade you. But, abbot, you must consider one thing. What you are saying now will not be understood by anyone.”

“...”

“Depending on how it is heard... yes. Depending on how it is heard... that...”

Jong Ri-Hyung hesitated slightly, bit his lips tightly, and then spoke vehemently.

“It also seems as if you are simply afraid of that Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

At that moment, a blade-like glare flashed from Beop Jeong’s eyes. Startled, Jong Ri-Hyung immediately bowed his head deeply and muttered.

“Of course, I do not think so... but those with short-sighted minds will only perceive it that way.”

“...”

“So, abbot, please consider once more. As a leader from above, you know well that sometimes the best choice is not the best. Thoughts that are too far ahead for others to understand only alienate followers and cause division.”

Jong Hi-Hyung left those final words and stood up.

“I will not coerce you. But, abbot, please think of the best result, not just the best process. Farewell.”

Jong Ri-Hyung left the room. Beop Jeong watched his back silently.

He knew.

Though he said those words, Jong Ri-Hyung was desperately trying to soothe the disciples and defend Beop Jeong. Even while struggling, he would probably try to help Beop Jeong somehow.

But knowing that, before gratitude, a sense of emptiness overwhelmed him.

“...I am afraid...”

It’s ridiculous. Such a ridiculous story.

Who is he? Beop Jeong, the current abbot of the thousand-year-old Shaolin. And now he is afraid of someone who is merely the third class disciple of Mount Hua? Not because of strength, but because he cannot understand his thoughts?

How could this not be laughable?

“Maybe that’s really the case... hahaha.”

Beop Jeong’s hollow laughter filled the room.

And for a long time after, no sound of chanting or beads rolling was heard from that room.

“...Ahem.”

Tang Gunak cleared his throat loudly. Just a little thought made it clear what the Heavenly Friends Alliance was probably thinking right now.

“...Then what about Shaolin...?”

“They’ll handle it themselves, I suppose. Do we really need to worry about that?”

Closing his eyes tightly at those words, Tang Gunak asked again subtly.

“But if what you say is true, don’t they have to vacate their seats for us to leave the Yangtze as well?”

“Eh, not necessarily. Actually, we’ve already gotten everything we wanted. We can leave anytime.”

“Huh? Earlier you said they decide it?”

“That’s a different matter. We have to stay here so those bastards can’t get out.”

“...Don’t tell me?”

Chung Myung nodded and chuckled.

“Just by sitting here long enough, those bastards are dying off. Why should I get up first? I’ll hold out until the very end.”

“...”

“Fighting isn’t about winning originally. It’s about making the ones you hate die.”

Looking at Chung Myung, Tang Gunak reconsidered.

‘Good thing he’s not an enemy to me.’

When he first met that man, if by chance they had become enemies, Tang Gunak himself would be suffering what the abbot Shaolin is facing now. It wouldn’t have ended with just a hole in his stomach.

“So let’s not worry about them. Watching intently won’t change anything. The real problem now isn’t the Four Sects Alliance but the Nine Great Sects. For a while, all they can do is point fingers and grumble from afar. The public sentiment is too bad to pick a fight now.”

“Probably so...”

Public sentiment isn’t a tangible power but subtly pushes people forward. Especially when there’s no justification, public sentiment often causes things to go wrong.

Moreover, since the abbot prioritizes face and justification, they won’t be able to move no matter what for a while.

‘But...’

Tang Gunak is a cautious man. He carefully pondered again and spoke.

“It’s not something to be so reassured about.”

“Huh?”

“Saying they can’t move now means they will move eventually. And if it’s the Shaolin’s abbot that I know, when the time comes, something truly massive...”

Tang Gunak stopped speaking and closed his mouth. His face showed clear embarrassment.

Chung Myung, squinting slightly, looked at him and finished the sentence.

“They’ll appear as enemies?”

Tang Gunak couldn’t say yes or no. Whatever he said, the impact of those words spoken here was too great.

‘Enemies?’

So far, Tang Gunak had clashed with Shaolin many times from the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s perspective. Sometimes supporting Mount Hua, sometimes by his own will.

But what he had done was just an extension of conflicts between sects, not truly seeing the Nine Great Sects or the Five Great Families as his enemies. Yet today, the word ‘enemy’ almost slipped naturally from his mouth.

Even against Shaolin.

‘Is this really okay?’

His perception had naturally changed. Perhaps it happened because the Heavenly Friends Alliance hadn’t clearly decided how to deal with the Nine Great Sects.

“...Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Yes, Family lord.”

“I have something I want to ask...”

At those words, Chung Myung shrugged.

“I know what you want to say, but this isn’t the right place, right?”

Seeing Chung Myung read his mind like a ghost, Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

“Hmm, I see.”

“Well, isn’t that up to them, not us? If you’ve watched, I’ve never treated them badly first.”

“...”

“Why do you say that?”

“No, it’s just...”

Tang Gunak, about to say something, just laughed.

“Well, everyone has their own perspective.”

“Glad you understand.”

It’s better not to think about this.

“Then let’s put that aside for now.”

Both those who understood and those who didn’t nodded at that. Chung Myung shifted his gaze.

“So.”

“Huh?”

Baek Cheon, suddenly targeted, blinked in confusion.

“Again.”

“...Why suddenly come at me?”

“It seems like only we’re talking. Don’t you have anything to say in here? Surely you weren’t thinking nothing.”

Unable to say “I wasn’t thinking anything,” Baek Cheon made a vague excuse.

“Even if I don’t say it...”

“Oh, someone else will say it?”

Chung Myung said in a dry tone.

“It’s the same story as always.”

“Huh?”

“When war starts, there’s no time to wait for someone to give orders.”

“...”

“You have to judge and act immediately. Even a moment’s hesitation can cost lives.”

Though said casually, the meaning was significant.

‘So we should naturally think, judge, and voice opinions regularly.’

Thinking even this obvious meeting was preparation for war brought tension and slight frustration.

“Then, since it came up... about the small and medium sects.”

“Why them? What I said earlier was just...”

“No. Not that. Instead of waiting for them to join after war breaks out, wouldn’t it be better to actively incorporate them?”

“Oh?”

Chung Myung looked at Baek Cheon with interest and asked.

“Why?”

“It’s been said. If the Heavenly Friends Alliance isn’t properly organized, they won’t know whom to follow. Conversely, if we don’t know who they are beforehand, it’ll be hard to give proper orders.”

“Oh...”

“Wow...”

At Yoon Jong and Jo Gul’s strong reactions, Baek Cheon narrowed his eyes.

“What? Why?”

“No, sasuk. We are not mocking...”

“It’s been a long time since I heard something like that from sasuk.”

“...”

“Come to think of it, sasuk used to be like this.”

“I even heard he was a talent to lead Mount Hua. Now he’s just a neighborhood bar...”

“...You’d better shut that annoying mouth for your own good.”

“Yes sir!”

Jo Gul immediately shut up. Baek Cheon gritted his teeth. Meanwhile, Yu Yiseol silently nodded in agreement, which irritated him.

“Not a bad idea.”

“Honestly, just waiting was passive from our side.”

Supportive opinions came from various places, especially Tang Gunak and Meng So.

“Precisely identifying those to lead is definitely important.”

“As Mount Hua’s Sword Saint said earlier, most still trust the Nine Great Sects a bit more, so it wouldn’t be bad to act proactively before they naturally accept the Nine Great Sects again as public sentiment dilutes.”

“Hmm. I agree. Baek Cheon gave a good suggestion.”

Baek Cheon felt a surge of confidence after a long time.

He had been treated like a neighborhood bar fool despite his skills. Being recognized by the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s senior members restored his old confidence.

‘Yes, this is it...’

“Ugh. Such a meaningless act.”

A voice suddenly interrupted, dashing his confidence. Baek Cheon turned to see Im So-Byeong still leaning against the wall, smirking.

“Ah, sorry, Taoist Baek Cheon. This junior sometimes gets carried away.”

Seeing Im So-Byeong smack his own mouth annoyingly made Baek Cheon even angrier.

“...Did you say it’s meaningless?”

“Yes. Well, it’s not that Taoist Baek Cheon’s idea is bad, but I keep thinking it’s a task that brings no gain and only headaches. I know that’s typical of the Justice faction... haha.”

“May I ask why you think it’s meaningless? To me, it seems clearly helpful.”

“It’s helpful, sure. What isn’t helpful in this world? Even if you open the door and sweep the yard, it helps living in the place.”

Might as well say it’s not helpful, damn it. Baek Cheon’s stomach ached. Chung Myung alone was hard to handle, and with Im So-Byeong too, his stomach ached from both sides.

“You think that way...”

“You may ask why? Of course. It’s not hard to answer.”

Im So-Byeong got off the wall and sat up straight. His fan naturally opened wide in his hand.

“Because the meeting lost its purpose from the start, these useless opinions come out.”

“...Huh?”

“We solved one thing, so let’s find another task. What’s the grand premise before that?”

“Well... to organize the Heavenly Friends Alliance more efficiently and fight the Evil Sect...”

“That’s the problem!”

Those nodding at Im So-Byeong’s words flinched.

“Talking such vague nonsense, the meeting just goes in circles! A waste of time!”

Those nodding shook their heads as if to say they never did that. The Five Swords, who had been nodding, sided with Im So-Byeong and began giving Baek Cheon reproachful looks.

Baek Cheon’s anger surged.

‘Those unloyal bastards...’

One day, I’ll beat you all before the Four Sects Alliance or anything else. Really...

“It’s not to confront the Four Sects Alliance.”

Smack!

Im So-Byeong struck his own hand with his folded fan.

“It’s to defeat the Four Sects Alliance.”

“...”

“When people do something, they feel relieved. They get comfort that they’re not idle and are trying. But that comfort sometimes ruins people. It wastes time on useless things.”

“Hmm...”

“I ask, so if we create new positions, can we defeat the Four Sects Alliance?”

Namgung Do-Wei flinched at Im So-Byeong’s sharp gaze.

“That is...”

“So? If we incorporate the small and medium sects and organize their structure, can we defeat the Four Sects Alliance?”

“Better than not doing anything...”

Baek Cheon trailed off. He also knew it wouldn’t greatly affect the war situation.

“Tsk tsk tsk tsk.”

Smack!

Im So-Byeong slapped his palm with his fan again and said.

“With people like these leading the Heavenly Friends Alliance is!”

Namgung Do-Wei and Baek Cheon’s faces flushed red. It was true and hard to refute, but the fact that Im So-Byeong said it made them even more embarrassed.

When have they ever been lectured by an Evil Sect bastard in their lives?

“Still, Namgung young lord is a bit better.”

“Huh?”

Namgung Do-Wei looked at Im So-Byeong in surprise. Could it be he was defending him...?

“It’s true that positions are needed. Instead of wasting time gathering these people for meetings, it’s better to create proper military positions and put me there! Then no need to rack bad brains, just roll as I command, ugh!”

Im So-Byeong was hit by a flying wooden pillow, rolled on the floor, and crashed into the wall.

“That Evil Sect bastard always crosses the line at every moment he finds.”

Chung Myung clapped his hands. Those staring blankly flinched as they saw Hyun Jong quietly withdraw his extended hand.

Huh? Come to think of it, that wooden pillow originally belonged to the... alliance leader...

Uh....

“But it’s not an unreasonable story.”

Chung Myung shrugged and looked at the wriggling Im So-Byeong.

“Alright, go ahead and talk. So what must we do to defeat the Four Sects Alliance?”

“While getting beaten like this?”

“Oh, want to get hit more then?”

“Ugh, damn.”

Im So-Byeong wiped the blood dripping from his nose with his sleeve, frowned, and straightened up again.

Everyone looked at his mouth with tense eyes.

In that atmosphere, even Chung Myung looked at Im So-Byeong with a bit of hope.

The reason he convened this meeting was not because he didn’t know what to do next. He had already finished thinking about what should be done first.

Nevertheless, there were two reasons why he hosted the meeting.

One was that he could no longer insist on thinking and leading alone.

As he just told Baek Cheon, each person here is someone who must judge and lead others when war breaks out.

If they get used to waiting for someone else to make decisions, there will inevitably be situations where they fail to judge properly when they must decide for themselves.

Therefore, at least they must become those who find the best course themselves. For that, even Chung Myung needs to reduce his own opinions a little.

Even if the meeting’s outcome contradicts what Chung Myung thinks is best, he believed it would ultimately be the best.

The other reason was....

‘I can’t do it alone.’

Chung Myung is not omnipotent.

No matter how many terrible wars he endured in the past or how many deadly situations others cannot even imagine he crossed, Chung Myung’s experience and perception have limits.

Until now, his way of thinking worked, but to face the huge enemies like Jang Ilso and the Demonic Cult, his strength alone is insufficient.

So he wants to gather opinions. Those here are worthy of Chung Myung’s attention.

At that moment, Im So-Byeong spoke.

“What is important to win?”

“...Yes?”

When Namgung Do-Wei asked blankly, Im So-Byeong tapped his own head with his folded fan.

“Maybe the question was a bit difficult. If two people fight, who wins?”

“Well... the one who trained a bit harder.”

“No, no. It’s the one who understands the opponent better.”

“I think rather the one who clearly knows their limits has a higher chance to win.”

Reasonable answers poured out.

Then, someone who gets hives from obvious words blurted out incredulously.

“Isn’t it just that the stronger one wins?”

A moment of silence fell. As everyone’s gaze focused on him, Jo Gul’s face flushed red.

“Ah, no, I mean...”

“That’s right!”

“...Huh?”

Whoosh!

Im So-Byeong opened his fan.

“No matter how you dress it up or talk around it, the conclusion is one: the stronger side wins. Isn’t that the only truth in the murim world?”

“See! I was right!”

“Who said anything about that?”

“Why are you getting so worked up alone?”

“Oh my, well done.”

“You guys!”

Everyone coldly averted their gaze from Jo Gul. There are always people you don’t want to praise even if they do well.

“Then how should we win in war, young lord of Namgung?”

Receiving that heavy gaze, Namgung Do-Wei leaned his upper body slightly back.

“Well...”

He never imagined such a ridiculous answer would come from his mouth, but the atmosphere forced him to give one answer.

“We just need to become stronger.”

“Oh dear. Now your brain’s working a bit.”

“...”

He gave the most foolish answer in his life and was praised for it. This plunged Namgung Do-Wei into an inexplicable self-loathing. Of course, he didn’t realize it was strange that he was secretly happy to be praised by Im So-Byeong, who is from the Evil Sect.

“Now, what are the ways to become stronger?”

“Well...”

Im So-Byeong apparently didn’t expect an answer here and neatly summarized.

“There are exactly two: improve quality or increase quantity.”

Baek Cheon snapped at that.

“No, then what about the small and medium sects earlier...”

“Just to clarify, increasing quantity means supplementing those who can be combatants. Surely no one here thinks that gathering all riffraff will make us stronger, right?”

“Of course not.”

“...”

Baek Cheon, who suddenly dodged the question, was met with sharp gazes. He bowed his head deeply and ground his teeth inwardly.

“But that...”

Tang Pae quietly spoke up.

“Those aren’t the only two methods. Strategy, tactics, and...”

“Ah, stratagem?”

Im So-Byeong snickered.

“Ah, that could be. If the family lord uses his head well, he might fight an enemy twice as strong.”

“It wouldn’t be easy but surely...”

“Then three times?”

“...”

“Five times? Ten times?”

Tang Pae was suddenly speechless. If using your head could let you beat someone ten times stronger, why would people fear climbing mountains at night? They’d just catch tigers with bare hands and progress up.

“Do you understand? Strategy and tactics are meaningless against a power gap. If you can win without thinking, why would you waste your mind trying to find ways? The body is weak, so the head struggles to cover it.”

“Ah, no... but Han Gaozu defeated Xiang Yu.”

“If it was one-on-one, he’d have been beaten to death! That guy gathered all available forces to gang up!”

“...”

Hearing that, it was indeed a valid point.

“Who is stronger now, the Four Sects Alliance or us?”

“Well...”

This is an answer anyone can give without thinking.

“The Four Sects Alliance.”

“Yes. They are stronger.”

With the current Heavenly Friends Alliance’s strength, it’s not easy to face the Four Sects Alliance alone. Of course, the situation depends on how well they coordinate, but based on revealed strength, the Four Sects Alliance clearly has the upper hand.

“Anyone with sense wouldn’t waste time devising plans to overcome stronger forces with weaker ones. Instead, they look for ways to increase their strength.”

Everyone began to be captivated by Im So-Byeong’s words.

“But what about the quality of troops!”

Im So-Byeong sharply turned his head and looked at Chung Myung.

“There’s someone here who handles that. That guy has a bad temper and is useless elsewhere, but this ability is top-notch... Hey, hey! Put that down. If you hit me with the inkstone, I’ll really die.”

Chung Myung reluctantly put down the inkstone he had lifted. Im So-Byeong wiped cold sweat with his sleeve and continued.

“So the only thing left is to increase quantity.”

“But...”

Hearing that, Tang Pae made a vague expression.

“If the small and medium sects don’t help, we have no choice but to bring in the major sects, but there’s nowhere to join us now, right?”

“There’s no surplus force either. Everyone has already merged and split.”

“Hmm.”

Everyone spoke with puzzled faces, and Im So-Byeong smiled meaningfully.

“Then everyone agrees that major sects are needed, right?”

“...If only it were possible.”

“Of course, if it were possible, yes, but realistically it’s difficult, isn’t it?”

Im So-Byeong’s point struck their sore spot precisely.

The reason the Heavenly Friends Alliance is trying to improve mutual understanding and strengthen cooperation now is twofold: one, to reduce casualties, and the other...

‘Because otherwise, we really can’t win.’

Baek Cheon looked at Im So-Byeong seriously. The most painful thing Im So-Byeong said so far was, “People feel relieved once they do something.”

That perfectly fit Baek Cheon. Though he recognized the gap between the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Four Sect Alliance, he focused all his mind on daily training, trying to do what he could rather than solving the fundamental problem.

‘Is this a difference in perspective?’

Perhaps this is the difference between Im So-Byeong, who thinks and tries to solve things himself, and Baek Cheon, who still waits for someone to provide solutions.

Baek Cheon deeply engraved this moment in his heart.

Im So-Byeong snapped his fan shut and said.

“Good. Beon Chung!”

“Yes, Green Forest King!”

“Spread the map!”

“Yes!”

Beon Chung spread the large map he held. Because of his large size, he could spread a map that usually required two people alone.

‘He prepared that in advance?’

Tang Gunak looked at Im So-Byeong in disbelief. It seemed the Green Forest King had anticipated this kind of conversation here from the start.

“Currently, there are four sects in the Central Plains without clear affiliation.”

“Four places?”

“Yes. Two of them you know: the remaining two of the Five Palace, The South Sea Sun Palace and the Potala Palace.”

“Ah...”

Those who heard this blinked. When Potala Palace was mentioned, Chung Myung’s eyes momentarily sparkled.

“And another is the Blood Palace, included when referring to the Outer Five Palaces.”

“B-but the Blood Palace is...”

“Yes, yes. I know. But for now, let’s set aside possibilities and discuss only unaffiliated forces.”

“Hmm. In that case...”

The Blood Palace is a mysterious sect whose headquarters are unknown, and its disciples are notoriously eccentric, so it doesn’t align with the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s way.

“Though it’s said the Outer and Central Plains don’t mix, that doesn’t apply to the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Fortunately, leaders of the two Palaces are here, so persuading them will be somewhat easier.”

“...Why didn’t I think of that?”

“Indeed...”

Everyone looked at each other in disbelief.

Of course, just because Beast Palace and Ice Palace are part of the Heavenly Friends Alliance doesn’t guarantee their support. The Five Palaces Beyond the Great Wall have no clear representative sect, and their cohesion is looser than expected.

But it’s also true that these two sects could fill the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s lacking strength.

“Among these, the most powerful is Potala Palace...”

“No.”

Chung Myung sharply waved his hand.

“A war will break out on the way from the Western Province here.”

“Correct.”

Im So-Byeong clicked his tongue regretfully.

“Moreover, Potala Palace rarely leaves its territory and is known not to intervene in other sects’ disputes unless it’s a major issue. So it’s unlikely they will join.”

The Five Swords recalled monk Lama they met before and nodded.

No, they probably couldn’t even ask that person to fight with them. That brief meeting showed he was different from ordinary warriors.

“Then the remaining place is here.”

Snap!

When Im So-Byeong lightly pointed to a spot on the map, everyone’s eyes widened.

“...You mean going all the way there?”

“Ah, no, do people even live there? Is the Central Plains that vast?”

“Ugh.”

Meng So scratched his head looking at the place Im So-Byeong pointed to.

“That’s South Sea Sun Palace.”

“Yes, it is a major sect currently unaffiliated with any place.”

“But... sigh. That’s not easy. Isn’t South Sea Sun Palace located in Lam Ap (Vietnam)?”

“That’s right.”

“Their language and script are different from ours. They won’t meet easily.”

“We have to accept that.”

“And... importantly, South Sea Sun Palace is not a sect wielding imperial power in the Five Palace, as you know.”

“Uh?”

“They’re the real royalty. The people of the South Sea Sun Palace are royal family members.”

“...Huh?”

Im So-Byeong blinked, apparently unaware.

“Really?”

“Why would I lie about this? So they won’t even consider coming to fight in a foreign land. How would they perceive the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s invitation?”

“Well...”

“Wouldn’t it sound like asking royal family members to become mercenaries in another country’s war?”

“Uh...”

“If they send an envoy, count me out. I don’t want to be torn apart far from home.”

Im So-Byeong was dumbfounded, staring blankly at Meng So.

Then Chung Myung spoke.

“You started grandly. So what? In the end, we can’t bring anyone in?”

“And then you dismiss everything arrogantly.”

“What? A meaningless act?”

As the atmosphere grew tense, Im So-Byeong hesitated and stepped back. Especially Namgung Do-Wei and Baek Cheon’s gazes were serious.

“Wait, wait! There’s still more!”

“What nonsense now!”

“Stop useless resistance and accept it quietly!”

“No, there is! I said Five Palace! This one is certain! Right here!”

Thud!

When Im So-Byeong pointed to another spot on the map, everyone’s gaze focused on his fingertip.

“...What?”

“Are you sane...?”

“Is this guy really crazy?”

Everyone simultaneously expressed disbelief.

When everyone seemed at a loss for words, there was one distinct reaction.

“Why is that?”

At that moment, Yoon Jong twisted Jo Gul’s chin without asking or questioning.

“Kyaul!”

Seeing Jo Gul fall instantly, Yoon Jong clicked his tongue shortly.

“Please, act like a human...”

But at that moment, Jo Gul suddenly stood up and shouted.

“Ah, it’s not me!”

“Huh?”

Yoon Jong blinked in confusion, and Jo Gul protested with a face full of grievance.

“No, sahyung! I’m still the son of a merchant family who made a living through trade; surely I know where that place is?”

“...Is that so?”

“Yes.”

“Ah, then that makes sense. That is an island, and the hatched area between the land and the island is the sea.”

“If it’s an island?”

“Southern Island.”

Yu Yiseol still looked puzzled. But even Tang Soso couldn’t solve this further. The reason Yu Yiseol was confused was why that man was pointing to the Southern Island, and even Tang Soso didn’t know the reason.

As Yu Yiseol’s blank gaze and Tang Soso’s suspicious look poured out simultaneously, Im So-Byeong smiled slyly.

“Hehehe. Yes, that’s right. This is the Southern Island.”

“...W-wait a moment.”

At that moment, Baek Cheon interrupted, looking incredulous.

“Southern Islands?”

“Yes!”

“Really the Southern Islands?”

“Are your eyes bad by any chance?”

“Ah, no, it’s not that...”

Baek Cheon couldn’t understand at all, but still asked with a questioning expression.

“Then perhaps... the great sect that the Green Forest King mentioned, the one which can join us? Surely not... right?”

“Hehe. Why not!”

Bang!

Im So-Byeong slapped the map with his palm and said triumphantly.

“There are countless sects in the world, but there is only one great sect based on the Southern Island in the southern sea!”

“...”

“Yes! Why hide it! It’s the Southern Island Sect!”

“Gul.”

“Yes, sahyung.”

“Do you know today’s lunch menu?”

“I think it was beef today.”

“Oh, good. Shall we go eat?”

“Let’s do that.”

“Then I should go train as well.”

“Ugh. Now that I think about it, I still have some work left. Let’s start with that...”

As people began to get up one by one with indifferent faces, Im So-Byeong shouted in an even louder voice.

“Ah, no, why such reactions! I brought such an amazing strategy!”

“I’m hungry.”

“Ah, my shoulder hurts from training yesterday.”

“Will you train in the evening too?”

“Listen to me!”

When Im So-Byeong shouted, Baek Cheon scratched his ear and replied gruffly.

“You have to make sense for me to listen.”

“That guy is done for. I thought he was smart for a while.”

“Bandits are all like that. If they were that smart, would they live by banditry?”

Criticism beyond reproach poured out. But at that moment, Im So-Byeong opened his fan wide, covered his lower face, and chuckled softly.

“Oh dear, oh dear...”

Seeing Im So-Byeong shaking his head, everyone frowned.

“Why is that guy acting like that again?”

“Leave him be. He seems a bit crazy.”

Regardless, Im So-Byeong did not come out of his own world.

“Hehe. A genius ahead of his time must always suffer from envy and misunderstanding. It’s natural, so I’m not particularly angry.”

“...Ah, yes, yes. I suppose so.”

“I said let’s eat. I’m hungry.”

As people really tried to leave, Im So-Byeong hurriedly lowered his fan and shouted.

“No! Just listen first!”

“Why listen? It’s going to be nonsense anyway.”

“No! Why do you only say no! People have to keep all possibilities open! That closed-minded prejudice and fixed ideas block so many good thoughts...!”

“Hey, you!”

Unable to hold back, Baek Cheon raised his voice sharply.

“You have to make sense! Southern Island Sect belongs to the Nine Great Sects, so why would they join us? Do you think they have no sense!”

“There are even two from the Five Great Families here! Have you ever thought that the Tang Family or Namgung would leave the Five Great Families to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“Huh?”

Im So-Byeong said firmly.

“If I said that just five years ago, I would have been certified as a madman anywhere in the world. It would have been lucky if they even treated me like a person!”

“Well...”

Baek Cheon trailed off. In fact, it wasn’t entirely wrong. They were in the middle of that flow, so everything felt natural.

But for those watching the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s moves from afar, wasn’t it also shocking that the Tang family or Namgung joined the Heavenly Friends Alliance?

“If the Five Great Families could do it, why can’t the Nine Great Sects? Do they think they were born wearing a golden crown? Damn it! If they are the Nine Great Sects, then they are! Why discriminate and ignore people...”

“Whoa, whoa, Green Forest King. That’s not it. You have to speak without bias.”

“Ah, right.”

When Beon Chung calmed him down, Im So-Byeong suddenly changed his expression. Those watching frowned even more.

“The Nine Great Sects are people who share the same table. Just because you were once part of the Nine Great Sects doesn’t mean you’ll be forever in there. Mount Hua disciples had to prove themselves...”

“What, you bastard?”

“Chung Myung!”

“Chung Myung! Hold on!”

“Get that man away! Quickly! Why bring up that past story suddenly and make the quiet guy have a fit!”

The terrified Five Swords and elders subdued Chung Myung, whose eyes were half-rolled back, emitting a manic frenzy.

“What? Kicked out of the Justice faction? You bandit bastard with a loose mouth!”

“I-I didn’t say kicked out...”

“But this bastard?”

“C-Calm down!”

“Hey, hey! Hold him tight! If he hits him now, Green Forest King is done for!”

Im So-Byeong broke into a cold sweat and stepped back.

“Ahem, anyway...”

Bang!

Im So-Byeong spread his palm wide and struck the Southern Island on the map again.

“To understand what I mean, you need to understand the current situation of the Southern Island Sect.”

“Do we have to listen?”

“Really?”

Though tired responses leaked here and there, Im So-Byeong steadfastly continued.

“The part to focus on is here.”

Im So-Byeong drew a long horizontal line across the center of the large map.

No one here didn’t know what that line meant. Drawing across the Central Plains. That meant the Yangtze River.

“Currently, the Central Plains are divided north and south by the Yangtze River. The south of the river is controlled by the Four Sects Alliance, and the north is dominated by the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families.”

“Hmm.”

As familiar topics came up, people gradually became interested.

“The Yangtze River flows through Sichuan and reaches the transverse mountain range that forms the boundary between Sichuan and Tibet. In other words, once the Yangtze is occupied, the path from south to north is completely blocked.”

“What obvious nonsense.”

“Then look. Where are the Nine Great Sects located?”

“Well...”

Baek Cheon, about to answer indifferently, suddenly showed a subtle expression.

“Shall we see?”

Im So-Byeong quickly took out a fine brush from his bosom, opened the cap, and dotted the map densely.

“Do you understand?”

Baek Cheon nodded silently. Im So-Byeong shrugged and said.

“As you can see, most sects belonging to the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families are concentrated north of the Yangtze River. Emei, Qingcheng sect, and Dian Cang are in Sichuan just above the Yangtze; Wudang is in Hubei above the Yangtze; Southern Edge, Mount Hua, and Shaolin are in Henan and Shaanxi above Hubei. There’s no need to mention Kunlun in Qinghai and Kongtong in Gansu.”

“That’s right.”

It was nothing new. It was already well known that the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were centered north of the river. No need to question it again.

Except for that one place.

Im So-Byeong fiercely drew Southern Island Sect below Guangxi Province with his brush.

“How about here?”

“...It’s south.”

Of course. The Southern Sea is at the southernmost tip of the Central Plains.

Yes. Only the Southern Island Sect exists in the south of the river. Not just anywhere in the south, but isolated alone in the South Sea beyond the Four Sects Alliance’s territory. They cannot receive help from any sect.

“As you know, the Nine Great Sects and the Four Sects Alliance have crossed a point of no return. It’s only a matter of when they reveal it to each other.”

“Well...”

“Then let me ask one thing.”

Im So-Byeong smiled, lifting one corner of his mouth.

“Where will the war begin?”

“...”

“Until now, everyone naturally thought the war would start at the Yangtze River. But... is that really so?”

Baek Cheon closed his mouth.

“Let me ask, would the Hegemony Lord start a war leaving enemies behind their backs?”

No. Everyone here could confidently say no. Jang Ilso would never leave those who could attack his back alive.

“I guarantee, the signal for war won’t come from the Yangtze River. It will be Southern Island Sect. To us, the Southern Island seems isolated and struggling alone, but to the Hegemony Lord?”

Chung Myung gently pushed away those pressing on him and sat upright.

“...You mean it looks like a separate special unit behind the lines?”

“Exactly. When they focus their forces on the front main line, it looks like a place that can suddenly strike into the Four Sects Alliance’s territory and destroy the rear.”

Im So-Byeong grinned.

“What do you think, taoist? If you were the Hegemony Lord, what would you do with Southern Island Sect?”

“I’m not that Evil Sect bastard Jang Ilso, so that question annoys me.”

“...”

“But...”

Chung Myung rested his chin on his hand, eyes shining.

“If I were Jang Ilso... no, even if I’m not, I wouldn’t just leave the Southern Island alone.”

“Indeed...”

“But saying the war starts there is wrong.”

“...Yes?”

At that moment, Chung Myung’s voice lowered softly.

“They would want to settle things first before starting the war.”

“...”

“Even right now.”

With those words, the atmosphere in the room chilled and sank.

A strange atmosphere settled inside the room.

Chung Myung stared intently at the Southern Island drawn on the map.

“Did I miss something?”

No, that wasn’t it. Even if in his time the Southern Island Sect wasn’t part of the Nine Great Sects, he couldn’t have missed the fact that now the Southern Island Sect belonged to the Nine Great Sects and was located on Southern Island.

Still, the reason this fact didn’t remain in Chung Myung’s mind was because the Nine Great Sects were never a subject he needed to keep track of or coordinate with.

“That’s the Great Bald One’s responsibility.”

It’s hard enough to fully manage the Heavenly Friends Alliance alone; how could he also oversee the affairs of the Nine Great Sects?

But now, none other than Im So-Byeong was pointing out this very issue. Indeed, this was something Chung Myung found hard to consider.

Others also seemed to realize this situation was more serious than expected, so they stopped criticizing Im So-Byeong and fell into deep thought with serious expressions.

Tang Gunak rubbed his chin and spoke.

“So that means... the Southern Island Sect is currently in a perilous situation?”

“That’s right.”

“But there’s something I don’t understand. Even so, aren’t the ones who should resolve this the Nine Great Sects, and not us?”

Everyone nodded at that, but Im So-Byeong smiled meaningfully and said.

“Let’s think about it differently.”

“Huh?”

“Let’s look from the Southern Island Sect’s perspective. Those folks were living well on Southern Island. But one day, suddenly, the Nine Great Sects including Shaolin and the Namgung family... no, the Five Great Families...”

Im So-Byeong glanced awkwardly at Namgung Do-Wei and continued.

“They were fighting the Pirates along the Yangtze River but were suddenly forced by the Evil Sect to sign the Southern Lands Non-Aggression Treaty without any consultation. How do you think the Southern Island Sect felt?”

“It was a bolt from the blue for them.”

“They probably thought, ‘Are these people crazy?’”

“If it were me, I’d have gone to set fire to the bald head.”

It was not something that required deep thought.

The Southern Land Non-Aggression Treaty was a humiliating pact for the other Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, forcing them to avoid the Southern territory due to the Evil Sect’s pressure. But in reverse, it was only humiliation, not a real threat.

But how about from the Southern Island Sect’s perspective?

With just that treaty, the Southern Island Sect unknowingly became surrounded and isolated by the Evil Sect.

“Wait a moment. But wasn’t it the same before?”

“No, it’s different.”

Im So-Byeong answered Jo Gul’s question bluntly.

“Of course, Gangnam was previously dominated by the Evil Sect. But the difference between the Shenzhou Five Sects and the Four Sects Alliance is whether they recognized each other as enemies or as allies.”

“Ah...”

“Even with the Shenzhou Five Sects era, the Evil Sect is strong. But it was difficult for them to show that strength because they were enemies to each other. We also fought a war against those damned Ten Thousand People Clan bastards, didn’t we?”

Tang Gunak nodded heavily at those words.

“That’s right.”

“So even if the Southern Island Sect was geographically isolated, since the Evil Sect wouldn’t unite, they didn’t feel much threatened. And if the Evil Sect did attack, the Namgung Family, Wudang Sect, and geographically closer sects in Sichuan would immediately come to support them, right?”

“That’s likely.”

Tang Gunak nodded as if it was obvious. Even the Tang family would have dropped everything and headed to the Southern land if such a situation occurred. It was practically a promise between the Justice faction and the Five Great Families.

“But the moment the Southern Land Non-Aggression Treaty was signed!”

Swish!

Im So-Byeong’s brush once again drew a line across the Yangtze River on the map.

“No matter what happens to the Southern Island Sect, the Nine Great Sects cannot support them.”

“Ah...”

Only then did everyone understand the pressure the Southern Island Sect must have felt.

“For three whole years...”

“Yes, that’s right. The Southern Island Sect endured it for a full three years with a heart drying of blood, constantly anxious about when the Evil Sect might target them. And finally! That long, long three years is coming to an end...”

“Plum Blossom Island has erupted.”

“Yes. It exploded with a bang.”

Everyone’s gaze momentarily shifted to one side. Namgung Do-Wei bowed his head deeply, his face flushed red.

‘I never even thought about it.’

Of course, even if they had thought ahead, they couldn’t have stopped Namgung Hwang, but they had not considered at all what position the Southern Island Sect would be in when attacking the Evil Sect.

“The conclusion left by the Plum Blossom Island incident is one. The Gangnam Non-Aggression Treaty will end now... but that’s all. From now on, neither side can set foot on the other’s land. Didn’t Shaolin prove that directly during the Hangzhou Fire?”

“That’s right.”

Baek Cheon nodded strongly.

Shaolin, at that time, had effectively announced to the world that the Justice faction was reluctant to set foot on land dominated by the Four Sects Alliance, treaty or not.

“Then what position is the Southern Island Sect in now?”

Baek Cheon looked at the map with a stern face. What position? Well...

“They must feel abandoned.”

“Exactly.”

Im So-Byeong clapped his hands sharply.

“If Wudang had even considered Southern Island’s position, the Southern island Non-Aggression Treaty should never have happened. And if Shaolin had considered Southern Island’s position, they should have somehow stepped onto Southern Island land during the Demonic Cult’s rampage to signal that the Nine Great Sects could head to Southern Land if civilians or allies were in danger.”

Im So-Byeong sneered coldly.

“But neither happened.”

People’s shoulders trembled slightly. Putting themselves in Southern Island Sect’s shoes, they deeply felt the despair the faction must have experienced.

“Moreover, the Southern Island Sect was the last to join the Nine Great Sects, so they must have had an inferiority complex. On top of that, they were treated as if they didn’t exist. In such a situation, do you think they truly believe that just by maintaining their position in the Nine Great Sects, Shaolin or Wudang would come running to help them?”

“They wouldn’t.”

“Yes. Unless they’re fools, they wouldn’t. Unless the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were as united as before, how many sects would stand against the Four Sects Alliance to save the distant Southern Island Sect in this current state of internal division? I can say for certain...”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes grew cold. There was a bitter sneer about the reality of the Justice faction that always preached righteousness.

“There won’t be any.”

No one dared to speak rashly. They wanted to deny it, but everyone knew the reality was just that.

“Has Southern Land grown that vast?”

Suddenly, the map looked endlessly vast. To support the Southern Island Sect, one would have to cross that wide Southern land region vertically.

“This is impossible.”

Baek Cheon’s lips trembled slightly.

Actually, Hangzhou isn’t that deep into the Southern Lands. The problem is the long horizontal journey along the Yangtze River, not that it’s far from the river itself.

But the Southern Island Sect is completely different. To support Southern island, one must cross Southern lands, essentially the Four Sects Alliance’s stronghold, all the way to the sea.

“No, even if you reach the sea...”

“How do you get to the island?”

“...That means you have to find a boat to the Southern Island in the Southern lands, which is controlled by the Four Sects Alliance.”

“Moreover, if we depart after hearing that war has broken out, it will be over before you arrive. So we have to go in advance, right?”

Im So-Byeong nodded strongly and summarized.

“Yes, that’s right. So to help Southern island, those conditions must be met. You must be able to head to the South Sea even if Southern Island hasn’t requested rescue, and after breaking through the Southern lands, teeming with the Four Sects Alliance, you must find a way to reach Southern Island.”

Hyun Jong looked at the map with a stunned expression.

‘This is...’

This was not simply a matter of scolding the Nine Great Sects. Realistically, it was no exaggeration to say there was no way to help Southern Island from here.

“Green Forest King, this is...”

“Yes, I know. But that’s why it’s meaningful.”

Swish!

Im So-Byeong snapped open his fan and began speaking.

“The Southern Island Sect hopes for rescue, but realistically cannot imagine anyone coming to save them. Therefore, they are prepared for a desperate last stand and to die with their jade seals.”

“Hmm.”

“But at that moment!”

Im So-Byeong’s brush swiftly drew a line crossing Gangnam and reaching Haenam.

“What if we could help?”

“...”

“In a situation like now, where the Nine Great Sects’ power is weakening and the benefits of the name are meaningless, it wouldn’t be a dream for the Southern Island to leave the Nine Great Sects and join the Heavenly Friends Alliance on their own accord.”

Bang!

Im So-Byeong struck the map so hard that a part of the sturdy cloth map tore with a ripping sound.

“Do you understand?”

“...”

“It’s a defection from the Nine Great Sects. Something that has never happened once in the history of the murim world! Ah... of course, this means the first time a sect leaves on its own. A sect that was expelled...”

“Wait, is that guy for real?”

“Hey, Chung Myung! Hold it in!”

“This isn’t the time for that!”

“I’m pissed too, but let’s hear everything first and then kill him!”

Hyun Jong pressed down on the struggling Chung Myung and urged Im So-Byeong.

“Please continue.”

“Yes.”

Im So-Byeong glanced at Chung Myung with a somewhat irritating expression and continued.

“It’s not an easy story, but if it succeeds, all the martial arts sects in the world will witness the Southern Island Sect, part of the Nine Great Sects, throwing off their position and joining the Heavenly Friends Alliance on their own.”

“...”

“Do you understand what that means? You all just said why would a small or medium sect join the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and even if you were a small sect, you’d stick with the Nine Great Sects. Why do I say that? Because it’s the Nine Great Sects! Their history and reputation are hard to overcome in a short time, no matter how strong the Heavenly Friends Alliance becomes. But!”

Snap!

Im So-Byeong folded his fan in one swift motion and smiled slyly.

“It’s the Southern Island. Not a clumsy small sect, but the Southern Island Sect, one of the Nine Great Sects... will abandon the Nine Great Sects and choose the Heavenly Friends Alliance, declaring to the world that the Heavenly Friends Alliance is the more valuable place.”

“...”

“And then?”

Squeeeeze.

The tip of Im So-Byeong’s fan pressed gently on a part of the map. That spot was none other than Mount Song, where Shaolin was located.

“The balance between the Nine Great Sects and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Screeech.

The fan moved sideways almost tearing the map. Everyone’s gaze followed the tip of the fan.

“Could change...”

Finally, the fan stopped. The long line it drew ended at Mount Hua in Shaanxi, where the Mount Hua faction was located.

“Couldn’t it?”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes gleamed with a chilling light.

“If that’s even possible, I think it’s worth trying no matter what sacrifices or costs it entails.”

Tap!

He lightly tapped his palm with the fan, smiled, and stepped back.

“That’s all.”

As Im So-Byeong finished speaking, a strange atmosphere began to fill the room—a mix of confusion, concern, and a peculiar excitement.

No one could easily speak. Even those who seemed ready to say something repeatedly opened their mouths but could not bring themselves to speak and closed them again.

Such was the tremendous impact left by Im So-Byeong’s words.

“...The South Sea...?”

“Breaking through the South of the River?”

Someone was aghast at the recklessness of the plan Im So-Byeong had proposed.

The Southern Land is literally the land of the Four Sects Alliance. It was never friendly to them in the past, and now it is truly a dragon’s den and a deadly place.

To support a sect located at the southernmost tip of such a place, is that realistically possible?

“How on earth will we get ships?”

“Supporting an island... It’s not even an island in the North.”

Someone focused on the location itself—Southern Island, where the Southern Island Sect is based.

Southern Island is a name very familiar to them, yet it also feels endlessly distant. While no martial artist would be unfamiliar with the name Southern Island, very few have actually visited the Island.

In a sense, although it is one of the Nine Great Sects, Southern Island Sect feels even more remote than the sects outside the Central Plains.

“Wait a moment... This can only happen if they want our support. If we blindly go south and they reject us, we might end up truly isolated.”

“That would be the worst.”

And someone else questioned whether the Southern Island Sect itself could be trusted.

However... amid these differing thoughts, the strongest thought dominating their minds was different.

“The Nine Great Sects...”

The moment Chung Myung spoke, everyone in the room turned to look at him. Receiving all eyes, Chung Myung smiled mysteriously.

“Are you saying a sect will leave the Nine Great Sects?”

“Yes, that’s right.”

“You mean they will leave the Nine Great Sects on their own and join the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“Exactly.”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung tapped his cheek with his finger resting on his chin. But contrary to his thoughtful appearance, he reached a conclusion faster than expected.

“Just thinking about it...”

The corners of Chung Myung’s mouth twisted wickedly.

“Feels damn good?”

This is something whose impact doesn’t even need to be considered.

“If it’s even possible. Yes, if it really happens, the repercussions would be terrifying. It would overturn the entire Central Plains.”

The long, long history of the murim world.

In all that time, no sect has ever kicked away their position in the Nine Great Sects on their own. In fact, martial artists probably never even considered such a possibility.

Because the symbolism of the name Nine Great Sects is that immense.

It is no exaggeration to say that the goal of every Justice faction in the murim world is to secure a place in the Nine Great Sects or the Five Great Families.

Even among Mount Hua disciples, some quietly await the day to return to the Nine Great Sects. Of course, if Shaolin holds full authority over the Nine Great Sects, they would refuse even if given a seat for free, but if circumstances change, few would openly reject it.

Thus, the name Nine Great Sects has been a symbol of the Justice faction and the last bastion protecting the Central Plains.

But now...

“I think I hear the sound of cracks forming in that solid status.”

The corners of Chung Myung’s mouth curled up even more.

If by some one in a thousand or one in ten thousand chance this really happens, the name Nine Great Sects can no longer remain a symbol of the Justice faction.

Yes. If it really happens.

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak, who mostly had a stern face, now wore an expression twice as serious as usual. This situation carried that much significance.

“If it happens as the Forest King said, surely... it will be a tremendous event.”

Everyone nodded as if entranced by those words.

“Even the Southern Island Sect is no ordinary force. It’s one pillar of the Nine Great Sects. At least, um...”

Tang Gunak trailed off, but Namgung Do-Wei, perceptive, finished the sentence he dared not say aloud.

“It’s a force so large it can’t be compared to the Namgung Family, which lost more than half its strength.”

“...That wasn’t the meaning I was going for.”

“Don’t worry about it, Family Lord. The truth is the truth.”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes did not waver at all. His gaze seemed to say, ‘What matters is not now, but later.’ Seeing that, Tang Gunak nodded.

“Comparing to the Namgung Family is excessive, but it’s true that there aren’t many sects with such power in the world. Especially if limited to those who might join the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Even if it’s the lowest seat in the Nine Great Sects?”

Tang Gunak shook his head at Jo Gul’s words.

“The Southern Island Sect was the last to join, but its strength can’t be said to be the weakest among the Nine Great Sects. Compared to sects like Kunlun, which were almost destroyed by the Evil Sect, it’s much stronger.”

“Oh, is that so? Then why...”

“That’s obvious.”

Chung Myung answered instead of Tang Gunak.

“Just because a stronger sect appears than those in the Nine Great Sects doesn’t mean the original sects can be kicked out. That wouldn’t be righteous.”

“But they did kick one out, didn’t we?”

“Kyaaaah!”

As Chung Myung’s eyes suddenly rolled back again, everyone glared daggers at Jo Gul. Normally, Chung Myung would blame him for flipping out over nothing, but this time, the one who provoked was clearly at fault.

“Ah, no, I’m also from Mount Hua, so I can say this, right...?”

“Gul, just shut your mouth.”

“Yes!”

Jo Gul quickly shrank into a corner, and Chung Myung clicked his tongue and continued.

“...Anyway! After all considerations, there’s no sect like the Southern Island Sect.”

“That’s right.”

Tang Gunak also fully agreed on this point.

“Strength is strength, but more important than strength is symbolism. For a sect like the Southern Island to choose the Heavenly Friends Alliance over the Nine Great Sects—does anyone not understand what that means?”

The room fell as silent as a mouse. Someone swallowing dryly sounded like thunder.

“Of course... no matter how big the Southern Island is, it’s still just one sect, so the world won’t be overturned by declaration alone. But at least...”

Tang Gunak’s firm gaze swept over everyone in the room.

“It can at least twist the direction of the current. Surely.”

Chung Myung, with his fingers interlaced behind his head, muttered indifferently.

“The perception has already changed since the Namgung Family joined the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Exactly.”

Tang Gunak snapped his fingers lightly, as if to focus everyone’s attention.

“It’s a bit awkward for me to say this, but honestly, it’s hard to group the Five Great Families and the Nine Great Sects at the same level. Of course, when Namgung Hwang was the head, the Namgung Family could rival any sect in the Nine Great Sects... but other sects honestly can’t be compared to the Nine Great Sects. Public perception also doesn’t place the Five Great Families on the same level as the Nine Great Sects.”

“That’s true.”

“That’s why it’s meaningful, isn’t it? It means the Heavenly Friends Alliance has finally surpassed the Five Great Families and can even embrace the Nine Great Sects. Perhaps...”

A smile similar to Chung Myung’s appeared on Tang Gunak’s lips.

“Through this opportunity, the Heavenly Friends Alliance might surpass the status of the Nine Great Sects.”

Those words were the decisive blow.

Surpassing the Nine Great Sects.

It was a task the Heavenly Friends Alliance must accomplish someday, but at the same time, it was so distant that it was hard to even discuss.

But now, the clue has begun to unravel from an unexpected place.

“...What really happens if that happens?”

Yoon Jong muttered blankly, and Jo Gul scoffed and replied.

“What do you mean what happens? The big bald head will steam like a boiling kettle. He’ll be like a heated stone!”

“...We might even see Beop Jeong die of a heart attack.”

“At that level, possession by the demon of greed is certain. The Shaolin abbot might even change.”

At that moment, Im So-Byeong slammed his fan down with a sharp sound.

Everyone fell silent and looked at Im So-Byeong. Though he had been outspoken before, at this moment his influence even surpassed Tang Gunak.

“It’s not just that.”

“Huh?”

“Tsk tsk. Think carefully. What is the biggest difference between the Nine Great Sects and the Heavenly Friends Alliance right now?”

“Attitude?”

“...That’s true, but...”

Im So-Byeong sighed and said.

“Why are they called the Nine Great Sects One Union?”

“What do you mean by that?”

“Because only a certain number of sects can earn that name. But not so with the Heavenly Friends Alliance. The Heavenly Friends Alliance has no limit on the number of sects. And it doesn’t have to be only great sects.”

“Ah...”

“Until now, countless sects hesitated out of deference to the Nine Great Sects, but the moment the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s status surpasses or even equals the Nine Great Sects, perceptions will change.”

Swish!

Im So-Byeong half-covered his face with his opened fan and smiled slyly.

“Rather than serving under the Nine Great Sects, it’s better to belong proudly to the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“...”

“Besides, I’ve already set a brilliant move in advance.”

“A brilliant move?”

“The Green Forest! Exactly, the Green Forest! Without the Green Forest in the Heavenly Friends Alliance, most sects wouldn’t dare approach the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Even if they said they’d accept everyone without discrimination, they’d think it was just talk. But now, the Evil Sect members have actually joined the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“...”

“Then everyone will think! We may be a bit weak, but at least we’ll be treated better than those Evil Sect bastards.”

“...Um, Green Forest King...? It’s not wrong, but... is it okay for you to say that?”

“It’s the truth, so what is wrong? Just because I shout doesn’t mean I’m treated like a person. If I am upset, I should have been born in the Namgung Family.”

“But why do you keep picking on us from the start...”

“Exactly!”

Swish!

Im So-Byeong suddenly raised his voice.

“Just one move! With this single move, we can change the entire landscape of the Central Plains. And if this one move succeeds!”

His eyes shone bright blue.

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance will gain at least the minimum strength to fight against the Evil Sect Alliance. If only this succeeds. Do you understand?”

Anyone who doesn’t understand the situation after hearing this has no right to be here.

“We must do it.”

“It’s something that must be done no matter what.”

“Then who will go?”

At the moment everyone vowed to succeed in this task.

A nonchalant voice echoed.

“I oppose it.”

“Huh?”

Everyone’s eyes slightly widened.

The reason for their surprise was one: the person opposing the plan was Chung Myung, who seemed the most likely to push it forward with strength.

“Huh?”

“You?”

As incredulous gazes poured in, Chung Myung’s face twisted sharply.

“Why? Am I not allowed to oppose?”

“Actually, that’s not the strange part.”

“No, if you think about it, it’s much stranger that you actually listen obediently to others.”

Everyone nodded in agreement at Baek Cheon’s final remark, an authority on Chung Myung studies.

“That’s right?”

“Exactly.”

“As expected of the sasuk.”

“These bastards?”

But when Chung Myung glared, everyone quickly averted their eyes. Even Tang Gunak, who had unknowingly lowered his gaze, flinched and swiftly returned his head to its place.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Yes.”

“What is your opinion on this?”

Then Chung Myung slightly raised his head and looked at the map.

“Hey, listen carefully.”

“Yes, brother!”

Beon Chung quickly spread the map wide and lifted it up. It looked much more disciplined than when the Green Forest King ordered it, but no one found it strange. When Chung Myung was involved, even odd things were accepted as normal.

Chung Myung scratched his ear and said.
